0001	import { runtimeConfig } from './config/runtime';
0002	
0003	interface IAppOption {
0004	  globalData: {
0005	    apiBaseUrl: string;
0006	    cloudbaseEnvId: string;
0007	    environmentKind: 'demo' | 'authorized' | 'unconfigured';
0008	  };
0009	}
0010	
0011	App<IAppOption>({
0012	  globalData: {
0013	    // WeChat external config carries only non-secret build values.
0014	    apiBaseUrl: runtimeConfig.apiBaseUrl,
0015	    cloudbaseEnvId: runtimeConfig.cloudbaseEnvId,
0016	    environmentKind: runtimeConfig.environmentKind,
0017	  },
0018	  onLaunch() {
0019	    // 初始化阶段不发起网络请求；环境状态由后续服务端配置确认。
0020	  },
0021	});

=== 文件：admin/src/App.vue ===
0001	<script setup lang="ts">
0002	import { computed, onMounted, onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue';
0003	import DesktopWidthNotice from '@/components/DesktopWidthNotice.vue';
0004	
0005	const width = ref(typeof window === 'undefined' ? 1440 : window.innerWidth);
0006	const blocked = computed(() => width.value < 1280);
0007	const updateWidth = () => {
0008	  width.value = window.innerWidth;
0009	};
0010	onMounted(() => window.addEventListener('resize', updateWidth));
0011	onUnmounted(() => window.removeEventListener('resize', updateWidth));
0012	</script>
0013	
0014	<template>
0015	  <DesktopWidthNotice v-if="blocked" :viewport-width="width" />
0016	  <router-view v-else />
0017	</template>

0177	    register_exception_handlers(app)
0178	
0179	    @app.middleware("http")
0180	    async def request_id_middleware(request: Request, call_next: Any) -> Any:
0181	        try:
0182	            request.state.request_id = resolve_request_id(request.headers.get("X-Request-Id"))
0183	        except ApiException as error:
0184	            request.state.request_id = f"req_invalid_{resolve_request_id(None)[4:]}"
0185	            response = _error_response(request, error)
0186	            response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = request.state.request_id
0187	            return response
0188	        response = await call_next(request)
0189	        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = request.state.request_id
0190	        return response
0191	
0192	    @app.get("/api/v1/health")
0193	    async def health(request: Request) -> ApiEnvelope[HealthData]:
0194	        settings = request.app.state.settings
0195	        data = HealthData(
0196	            service="xinyu-v2-backend",
0197	            version="0.1.0",
0198	            environment_kind=settings.environment_kind.value,
0199	            status="ok" if settings.configuration_status == "ready" else "degraded",
0200	        )
0201	        return ApiEnvelope.success(request_id=request.state.request_id, data=data)
0202

0211	    app.include_router(treehole_student_router)

0213	    return app

=== 文件：miniprogram/pages/identity-verification/index.ts ===
0001	import { verifyIdentity } from '../../services/auth';
0002	
0003	Page({
0004	  data: { name: '', studentNumber: '', loading: false, error: '', from: '' },
0005	  onLoad(query: Record<string, string>) {
0006	    this.setData({ from: query.from ?? '' });
0007	  },
0008	  onNameInput(event: WechatMiniprogram.Input) {
0009	    this.setData({ name: event.detail.value, error: '' });
0010	  },
0011	  onStudentInput(event: WechatMiniprogram.Input) {
0012	    this.setData({ studentNumber: event.detail.value, error: '' });
0013	  },
0014	  async submit() {
0015	    if (!this.data.name.trim() || !this.data.studentNumber.trim()) {
0016	      this.setData({ error: '请填写姓名和学号' });
0017	      return;
0018	    }
0019	    this.setData({ loading: true, error: '' });
0020	    try {
0021	      await verifyIdentity(this.data.name.trim(), this.data.studentNumber.trim());
0022	      wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/today/index' });
0023	    } catch (error) {
0024	      this.setData({
0025	        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : '身份核验服务暂时不可用',
0026	      });
0027	    } finally {
0028	      this.setData({ loading: false });
0029	    }
0030	  },
0031	  cancel() {
0032	    wx.navigateBack({ fail: () => wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/today/index' }) });
0033	  },
0034	});

0022	  async begin(event: WechatMiniprogram.BaseEvent) {
0023	    const key = (event.currentTarget as WechatMiniprogram.Target).dataset
0024	      .key as AssessmentModule['key'];
0025	    const current = sessionStore.get();
0026	    if (!current?.identityVerified) {
0027	      wx.navigateTo({
0028	        url: `/pages/identity-verification/index?from=assessment-${key}`,
0029	      });
0030	      return;
0031	    }
0032	    if (current.accountStatus === 'recovery') {

0035	    }
0036	    try {

0038	      wx.navigateTo({

0041	    } catch (error) {
0042	      this.setData({

0014	    });
0015	  },
0016	  async remove() {
0017	    if (!this.data.id) return;
0018	    this.setData({ loading: true, error: '' });
0019	    try {
0020	      await deleteHistoryItem(this.data.id, this.data.category);
0021	      wx.navigateBack();
0022	    } catch (error) {
0023	      this.setData({
0024	        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : '删除没有成功，请稍后重试',
0025	      });
0026	    } finally {
0027	      this.setData({ loading: false });
0028	    }
0029	  },
0030	  cancel() {
0031	    wx.navigateBack();
0032	  },
0033	});

=== 文件：miniprogram/components/assessment-progress/index.ts ===
0001	Component({
0002	  properties: {
0003	    current: { type: Number, value: 0, observer: 'syncSegments' },
0004	    total: { type: Number, value: 0, observer: 'syncSegments' },
0005	  },
0006	  data: { segments: [] as number[] },
0007	  lifetimes: {
0008	    attached() {
0009	      this.syncSegments();
0010	    },
0011	  },
0012	  methods: {
0013	    syncSegments() {
0014	      const total = Math.max(0, Number(this.data.total) || 0);
0015	      this.setData({ segments: Array.from({ length: total }, (_, index) => index) });
0016	    },
0017	  },
0018	});

=== 文件：miniprogram/components/bottom-nav/index.ts ===
0001	Component({
0002	  properties: { current: { type: String, value: 'today' } },
0003	  data: {
0004	    items: [
0005	      { key: 'today', label: '今日' },
0006	      { key: 'assessment', label: '自测' },
0007	      { key: 'treehole', label: '树洞' },
0008	      { key: 'my', label: '我的' },
0009	    ],
0010	  },
0011	  methods: {
0012	    navigate(event: WechatMiniprogram.BaseEvent) {
0013	      const key = (event.currentTarget as WechatMiniprogram.Target).dataset
0014	        .key as string;
0015	      const routes: Record<string, string> = {
0016	        today: '/pages/today/index',
0017	        assessment: '/pages/assessment-center/index',
0018	        treehole: '/pages/treehole/index',
0019	        my: '/pages/my/index',
0020	      };
0021	      if (routes[key] && key !== this.data.current)
0022	        wx.switchTab({ url: routes[key] });
0023	    },
0024	  },
0025	});

=== 文件：miniprogram/config/local-preview.ts ===
0001	/** Local documentation preview only; never used outside WeChat DevTools. */
0002	export const localPreviewConfig = {
0003	  enabled: true,
0004	  apiBaseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:9000/api/v1',
0005	  cloudbaseEnvId: 'local-manual-demo',
0006	  environmentKind: 'demo',
0007	} as const;

=== 全文第 44 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：miniprogram/config/runtime.ts ===
0001	/** Non-secret values supplied by the WeChat external configuration. */
0002	import { localPreviewConfig } from './local-preview';
0003	
0004	export type RuntimeEnvironmentKind = 'demo' | 'authorized' | 'unconfigured';
0005	
0006	export interface RuntimeConfig {
0007	  apiBaseUrl: string;
0008	  cloudbaseEnvId: string;
0009	  environmentKind: RuntimeEnvironmentKind;
0010	}
0011	
0012	const isEnvironmentKind = (value: unknown): value is RuntimeEnvironmentKind =>
0013	  value === 'demo' || value === 'authorized' || value === 'unconfigured';
0014	
0015	const readExternalConfig = (): Record<string, unknown> => {
0016	  if (typeof wx.getExtConfigSync !== 'function') return {};
0017	  try {
0018	    const value = wx.getExtConfigSync();
0019	    return value && typeof value === 'object'
0020	      ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
0021	      : {};
0022	  } catch {
0023	    return {};
0024	  }
0025	};
0026	
0027	const external = readExternalConfig();
0028	try {
0029	  if (
0030	    Object.keys(external).length === 0 &&
0031	    localPreviewConfig.enabled &&
0032	    wx.getDeviceInfo().platform === 'devtools'
0033	  ) {
0034	    Object.assign(external, localPreviewConfig);
0035	  }
0036	} catch {
0037	  /* Unsupported clients keep the explicit unconfigured state. */
0038	}
0039	const apiBaseUrl =
0040	  typeof external.apiBaseUrl === 'string' ? external.apiBaseUrl.trim() : '';
0041	const cloudbaseEnvId =
0042	  typeof external.cloudbaseEnvId === 'string' ? external.cloudbaseEnvId.trim() : '';

0043	    Record<string, unknown> | Array<Record<string, unknown>>
0044	  >('/assessment-modules');

0046	    throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? '自测目录暂时不可用');

0048	};

0050	export const startAssessment = async (
0051	  module: AssessmentModule['key'],
0052	): Promise<AssessmentSession> => {
0053	  const idempotencyKey = `assessment-start-${module}-${Date.now()}`;
0054	  const result = await request<Record<string, unknown>>('/assessment-sessions', {
0055	    method: 'POST',
0056	    data: { module_code: apiModuleCode(module), client_start_key: idempotencyKey },
0057	    idempotencyKey,
0058	  });
0059	  if (result.error || !result.data)
0060	    throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? '暂时无法开始本次观察');
0061	  const data = result.data;
0062	  const questions = Array.isArray(data.questions)
0063	    ? (data.questions as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
0064	    : [];
0065	  const session: AssessmentSession = {
0066	    id: String(data.session_id ?? data.id ?? ''),
0067	    module,
0068	    title: String(data.title ?? displayModuleTitle(module)),
0069	    questionnaireVersion: String(data.questionnaire_version ?? 'v1'),
0070	    questions: questions.map((question, index) => {
0071	      const options = Array.isArray(question.options) ? question.options : [];
0072	      return {
0073	        id: String(question.question_key ?? question.id ?? `${module}-q${index + 1}`),
0074	        prompt: String(question.prompt ?? question.text ?? `第 ${index + 1} 题`),
0075	        options: options.map((option) =>
0076	          option && typeof option === 'object'
0077	            ? String((option as Record<string, unknown>).label ?? '')
0078	            : String(option),
0079	        ),
0080	        optionKeys: options.map((option, optionIndex) =>

=== 全文第 75 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：miniprogram/services/assessment.ts ===
0081	          option && typeof option === 'object'
0082	            ? String((option as Record<string, unknown>).option_key)
0083	            : String(optionIndex),
0084	        ),
0085	      };
0086	    }),
0087	  };
0088	  activeSessions.set(session.id, {
0089	    questions: session.questions,
0090	    version: Number(data.object_version ?? 1),
0091	  });
0092	  return session;
0093	};

0190	const normalizeAiAssist = (value: unknown): AssessmentAiAssist | undefined => {
0191	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return undefined;
0192	  const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;
0193	  const output = data.output_projection ?? data.output ?? data;
0194	  if (!output || typeof output !== 'object') return undefined;
0195	  const projection = output as Record<string, unknown>;
0196	  const status =
0197	    data.output_status === 'fallback' || data.status === 'fallback'
0198	      ? 'fallback'
0199	      : data.output_status === 'adopted' ||
0200	          data.status === 'adopted' ||
0201	          projection.status === 'ok'
0202	        ? 'adopted'
0203	        : undefined;
0204	  if (!status) return undefined;
0205	  return {
0206	    status,
0207	    summary: typeof projection.summary === 'string' ? projection.summary : undefined,
0208	    observations: Array.isArray(projection.observations)
0209	      ? projection.observations.filter(
0210	          (item): item is string => typeof item === 'string',
0211	        )
0212	      : undefined,
0213	    practicalSteps: Array.isArray(projection.practical_steps)
0214	      ? projection.practical_steps.filter(
0215	          (item): item is string => typeof item === 'string',
0216	        )
0217	      : undefined,
0218	    boundaryNotice:
0219	      typeof projection.boundary_notice === 'string'
0220	        ? projection.boundary_notice
0221	        : undefined,
0222	    fallbackCopy:
0223	      typeof data.fallback_copy === 'string' ? data.fallback_copy : undefined,
0224	  };
0225	};

0005	export const loginWithWechat = async (): Promise<StudentSession> => {
0006	  const loginResult = await new Promise<WechatMiniprogram.LoginSuccessCallbackResult>(
0007	    (resolve, reject) => {
0008	      wx.login({ success: resolve, fail: reject });
0009	    },
0010	  ).catch(
0011	    () => ({ code: 'demo-code' }) as WechatMiniprogram.LoginSuccessCallbackResult,
0012	  );
0013	  const result = await request<Record<string, unknown>>('/auth/wechat/session', {
0014	    method: 'POST',
0015	    data: { code: loginResult.code, client_version: 'xinyu-v2-miniprogram' },
0016	  });
0017	  if (result.error || !result.data)
0018	    throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? '暂时无法进入，请稍后重试');
0019	  const data = result.data;
0020	  const session: StudentSession = {
0021	    accessToken: String(data.access_token ?? data.accessToken ?? ''),
0022	    expiresAt: String(data.access_expires_at ?? data.expiresAt ?? ''),
0023	    identityVerified:
0024	      data.identity_status === 'verified' || data.identityVerified === true,
0025	    basicConsent:
0026	      data.base_consent_status === 'accepted' || data.basicConsent === true,
0027	    communityConsent:
0028	      data.community_consent_status === 'accepted' || data.communityConsent === true,
0029	    accountStatus: data.account_status === 'recovery' ? 'recovery' : 'active',
0030	  };
0031	  sessionStore.set(session);
0032	  sessionStore.setUser({
0033	    displayName: null,
0034	    accountStatus: session.accountStatus,
0035	    recoveryUntil: null,
0036	    basicConsent: session.basicConsent,
0037	    communityConsent: session.communityConsent,
0038	    identityVerified: session.identityVerified,
0039	  });
0040	  return session;
0041	};

=== 文件：miniprogram/services/display-time.ts ===
0001	export const displayTime = (raw: string, now = new Date()): string => {
0002	  const date = new Date(raw);
0003	  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
0004	  const minutes = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now.getTime() - date.getTime()) / 60000));
0005	  if (minutes < 1) return '刚刚';
0006	  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes} 分钟前`;
0007	  if (minutes < 1440) return `${Math.floor(minutes / 60)} 小时前`;
0008	  return `${date.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() ? '' : `${date.getFullYear()}年`}${date.getMonth() + 1}月${date.getDate()}日`;
0009	};

0323	export const request = <T,>(
0324	  path: string,
0325	  options: RequestOptions = {},
0326	): Promise<ApiEnvelope<T>> => {
0327	  const app = getApp<{ globalData: { apiBaseUrl?: string } }>();
0328	  const baseUrl = app.globalData.apiBaseUrl ?? '';
0329	  if (getEnvironment() === 'demo' && !baseUrl) {
0330	    return Promise.resolve(demoResponse<T>(path, options));
0331	  }
0332	  if (!baseUrl)
0333	    return Promise.resolve(
0334	      envelope<T>(
0335	        null,
0336	        apiError('UNAVAILABLE', '当前环境尚未配置学生端服务，请稍后重试。', true),
0337	      ),
0338	    );
0339	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
0340	    wx.request<ApiEnvelope<T>>({
0341	      url: `${baseUrl}${path}`,
0342	      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
0343	      data: options.data,
0344	      timeout: 8000,
0345	      header: {
0346	        Authorization: sessionStore.accessToken()
0347	          ? `Bearer ${sessionStore.accessToken()}`
0348	          : '',
0349	        'X-Request-ID': newRequestId(),
0350	        ...(options.idempotencyKey
0351	          ? { 'Idempotency-Key': options.idempotencyKey }
0352	          : {}),
0353	      },
0354	      success: (response) => {
0355	        const result =
0356	          response.data ??
0357	          envelope<T>(
0358	            null,
0359	            apiError('UNAVAILABLE', '服务返回暂时不可用，请稍后重试。', true),
0360	          );
0361	        if (result.error?.code === 'UNAUTHORIZED') sessionStore.clear();

=== 全文第 90 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：miniprogram/services/http.ts ===
0362	        resolve(result);
0363	      },
0364	      fail: () =>
0365	        resolve(
0366	          envelope<T>(
0367	            null,
0368	            apiError('NETWORK_ERROR', '网络暂时不可用，请稍后重试。', true),
0369	          ),
0370	        ),
0371	    });
0372	  });

0036	          summary: String(item.fixed_summary ?? item.summary ?? '观察结果'),

0001	export const MOOD_OPTIONS = [
0002	  { code: 'pleasant', label: '愉快' },
0003	  { code: 'calm', label: '平静' },
0004	  { code: 'tired', label: '疲惫' },
0005	  { code: 'anxious', label: '焦虑' },
0006	  { code: 'low', label: '低落' },
0007	  { code: 'irritable', label: '烦躁' },
0008	] as const;
0009	
0010	const MOOD_LABELS: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
0011	  MOOD_OPTIONS.map((item) => [item.code, item.label]),
0012	);
0013	
0014	export const moodLabel = (value: unknown): string => {
0015	  const code = String(value ?? '');
0016	  return MOOD_LABELS[code] ?? code;
0017	};
0018	
0019	export const formatMoodTime = (value: unknown): string => {
0020	  const raw = String(value ?? '');
0021	  const date = new Date(raw);
0022	  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return raw;
0023	  const shanghai = new Date(date.getTime() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
0024	  return `${String(shanghai.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(shanghai.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
0025	};
0026	
0027	export const moodDateKey = (value: Date = new Date()): string => {
0028	  // The API defines each recording day in Asia/Shanghai.
0029	  return new Date(value.getTime() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
0030	};

0010	type UnknownRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
0011	
0012	const record = (value: unknown): UnknownRecord =>
0013	  value && typeof value === 'object' ? (value as UnknownRecord) : {};
0014	const listFrom = (value: unknown, key: string): UnknownRecord[] => {
0015	  if (Array.isArray(value))
0016	    return value.filter((item): item is UnknownRecord =>
0017	      Boolean(item && typeof item === 'object'),
0018	    );
0019	  const wrapped = record(value)[key];
0020	  if (!Array.isArray(wrapped)) throw new Error('内容暂时无法读取，请重新试试。');
0021	  return wrapped.filter((item): item is UnknownRecord =>
0022	    Boolean(item && typeof item === 'object'),
0023	  );
0024	};
0025

0051	          item.lastCompletedAt ??

0032	    recentObservation:
0033	      observations[0]?.summary ??
0034	      (data.recent_observation ? String(data.recent_observation) : null),
0035	    observations,
0036	  };
0037	};
0038	
0039	export const saveMood = async (mood: string): Promise<MoodRecord> => {
0040	  const recordDate = moodDateKey();
0041	  const result = await request<Record<string, unknown>>('/moods/today', {
0042	    method: 'PUT',
0043	    data: { record_date: recordDate, mood_code: mood, object_version: 1 },
0044	    idempotencyKey: `mood-${recordDate}`,
0045	  });
0046	  if (result.error || !result.data)
0047	    throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? '没有保存成功，请再试一次');
0048	  const data = result.data;
0049	  return {
0050	    id: String(data.record_id ?? data.id ?? ''),
0051	    mood: moodLabel(data.mood_code ?? data.mood ?? mood),
0052	    recordedAt: formatMoodTime(
0053	      data.saved_at ?? data.recordedAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
0054	    ),
0055	  };
0056	};

0022	export const publishPost = async (body: string): Promise<TreeholePost> => {
0023	  const idempotencyKey = `post-${Date.now()}`;
0024	  const result = await request<Record<string, unknown>>('/treehole/posts', {
0025	    method: 'POST',
0026	    data: { body, client_idempotency_key: idempotencyKey },
0027	    idempotencyKey,
0028	  });
0029	  if (result.error || !result.data)
0030	    throw new Error(result.error?.message ?? '内容没有提交成功，请稍后重试');
0031	  const data = result.data;
0032	  return normalizeTreeholePost(data.display_projection ?? data);
0033	};

0035	export const respondToPost = async (id: string, body: string): Promise<void> => {
0036	  const idempotencyKey = `response-${id}-${Date.now()}`;
0037	  const result = await request(`/treehole/posts/${id}/responses`, {
0038	    method: 'POST',
0039	    data: { body, object_version: 1, client_idempotency_key: idempotencyKey },
0040	    idempotencyKey,
0041	  });
0042	  if (result.error) throw new Error(result.error.message);
0043	};

0047	    method: 'DELETE',

0007	export interface LocalAssessmentState {
0008	  sessionId: string;
0009	  module: AssessmentModule['key'];
0010	  title: string;
0011	  questions: AssessmentQuestion[];
0012	  answers: number[];
0013	  currentIndex: number;
0014	  safetyState?: 'can_be_safe' | 'uncertain' | 'cannot_be_safe';
0015	  result?: AssessmentResult;
0016	}
0017	
0018	let state: LocalAssessmentState | null = null;
0019	
0020	export const assessmentStore = {
0021	  start(
0022	    next: Omit<LocalAssessmentState, 'answers' | 'currentIndex'>,
0023	  ): LocalAssessmentState {
0024	    state = { ...next, answers: [], currentIndex: 0 };
0025	    return state;
0026	  },
0027	  get(): LocalAssessmentState | null {
0028	    return state;
0029	  },
0030	  setAnswer(index: number, value: number): void {
0031	    if (!state) return;
0032	    state.answers[index] = value;
0033	    state.currentIndex = index;
0034	  },
0035	  setSafety(value: LocalAssessmentState['safetyState']): void {
0036	    if (state) state.safetyState = value;
0037	  },
0038	  setResult(result: AssessmentResult): void {
0039	    if (state) state.result = result;
0040	  },
0041	  clear(): void {
0042	    state = null;
0043	  },
0044	};

0002	import AdminLoginView from '@/views/AdminLoginView.vue';
0003	import WorkbenchView from '@/views/WorkbenchView.vue';
0004	import TaskDetailView from '@/views/TaskDetailView.vue';
0005	import AuditDemoView from '@/views/AuditDemoView.vue';

0007	
0008	const router = createRouter({
0009	  history: createWebHistory(),
0010	  routes: [
0011	    { path: '/login', component: AdminLoginView, meta: { guestOnly: true } },
0012	    { path: '/', component: WorkbenchView, meta: { requiresAuth: true } },
0013	    {
0014	      path: '/tasks/:taskId',
0015	      component: TaskDetailView,
0016	      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
0017	    },
0018	    { path: '/audit', component: AuditDemoView, meta: { requiresAuth: true } },
0019	  ],
0020	});
0021	
0022	router.beforeEach((to) => {
0023	  const session = useAdminSessionStore();
0024	  if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !session.isAuthenticated) return '/login';
0025	  if (to.meta.guestOnly && session.isAuthenticated) return '/';
0026	  return true;
0027	});
0028	
0029	export default router;

0001	import { apiEnvelopeSchema } from '@/schemas/api';
0002	import { adminRuntime } from '@/config/runtime';
0003	
0004	export class AdminApiError extends Error {
0005	  readonly code: string;
0006	  readonly retryable: boolean;
0007	  readonly currentVersion: number | null;
0008	  readonly requestId: string | null;
0009	  readonly status: number;
0010	
0011	  constructor(
0012	    message: string,
0013	    options: {
0014	      code?: string;
0015	      retryable?: boolean;
0016	      currentVersion?: number | null;
0017	      requestId?: string | null;
0018	      status?: number;
0019	    } = {},
0020	  ) {
0021	    super(message);
0022	    this.name = 'AdminApiError';
0023	    this.code = options.code ?? 'INTERNAL_ERROR';
0024	    this.retryable = options.retryable ?? false;
0025	    this.currentVersion = options.currentVersion ?? null;
0026	    this.requestId = options.requestId ?? null;
0027	    this.status = options.status ?? 500;
0028	  }
0029	}
0030	
0031	const API_BASE = adminRuntime.apiBaseUrl;
0032	
0033	export function createIdempotencyKey(): string {
0034	  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && 'randomUUID' in crypto) {
0035	    return crypto.randomUUID();
0036	  }
0037	  return `admin-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
0038	}
0039	
0040	export async function request<T>(
0041	  path: string,
0042	  options: {
0043	    token?: string | null;
0044	    method?: 'GET' | 'POST';
0045	    body?: unknown;
0046	    idempotent?: boolean;
0047	    signal?: AbortSignal;
0048	    fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
0049	  } = {},
0050	  parse: (value: unknown) => T,
0051	): Promise<{ data: T; requestId: string }> {
0052	  const headers = new Headers({ Accept: 'application/json' });
0053	  if (options.body !== undefined) headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
0054	  if (options.token) headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${options.token}`);

=== 全文第 127 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：admin/src/services/apiClient.ts ===
0055	  if (options.idempotent) headers.set('Idempotency-Key', createIdempotencyKey());
0056	
0057	  let response: Response;
0058	  try {
0059	    response = await (options.fetchImpl ?? fetch)(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
0060	      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
0061	      headers,
0062	      body: options.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(options.body),
0063	      signal: options.signal,
0064	    });
0065	  } catch {
0066	    throw new AdminApiError('暂时没有连接到服务，请重新尝试', {
0067	      code: 'DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE',
0068	      retryable: true,
0069	      status: 503,
0070	    });
0071	  }
0072	
0073	  const raw: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
0074	  const envelope = apiEnvelopeSchema.safeParse(raw);
0075	  if (!envelope.success) {
0076	    throw new AdminApiError('暂时没有加载到内容，请重新尝试', {
0077	      code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR',
0078	      retryable: true,
0079	      status: response.status,
0080	    });
0081	  }
0082	  if (!response.ok || envelope.data.error) {
0083	    const error = envelope.data.error;
0084	    throw new AdminApiError(error?.message ?? '暂时没有完成这项操作，请重新尝试', {
0085	      code: error?.code,
0086	      retryable: error?.retryable,
0087	      currentVersion: error?.current_version,
0088	      requestId: envelope.data.request_id,
0089	      status: response.status,
0090	    });
0091	  }
0092	  return {
0093	    data: parse(envelope.data.data),
0094	    requestId: envelope.data.request_id,
0095	  };
0096	}

0010	export const useAdminSessionStore = defineStore('adminSession', () => {
0011	  const initial = getInitialAdminSession();
0012	  const state = reactive({
0013	    isAuthenticated: false,
0014	    displayName: initial.displayName,
0015	    capabilityLabel: initial.capabilityLabel,
0016	    environmentKind: initial.environmentKind,
0017	    accessToken: '',
0018	    refreshToken: '',
0019	    accessExpiresAt: '',
0020	    refreshExpiresAt: '',
0021	    sessionExpiresAt: '',
0022	  });
0023	
0024	  function setSession(session: AdminSessionSummary): void {
0025	    state.isAuthenticated = true;
0026	    state.displayName = session.displayName;
0027	    state.capabilityLabel = session.capabilityLabel;
0028	    state.environmentKind = session.environmentKind;
0029	    state.accessToken = session.accessToken;
0030	    state.refreshToken = session.refreshToken;
0031	    state.accessExpiresAt = session.accessExpiresAt;
0032	    state.refreshExpiresAt = session.refreshExpiresAt;
0033	    state.sessionExpiresAt = session.sessionExpiresAt ?? session.accessExpiresAt;
0034	  }
0035	
0036	  async function hydrate(): Promise<boolean> {
0037	    if (!state.accessToken) return false;
0038	    try {
0039	      const me = await loadAdminMe(state.accessToken);
0040	      state.displayName = me.displayName;
0041	      state.capabilityLabel = me.capabilityLabel;
0042	      state.environmentKind = me.environmentKind;
0043	      state.sessionExpiresAt = me.sessionExpiresAt ?? state.accessExpiresAt;
0044	      state.isAuthenticated = true;
0045	      return true;
0046	    } catch {
0047	      clear();
0048	      return false;
0049	    }
0050	  }
0051	
0052	  async function logout(): Promise<void> {
0053	    if (state.accessToken)
0054	      await logoutAdmin(state.accessToken).catch(() => undefined);
0055	    clear();
0056	  }
0057	
0058	  function clear(): void {
0059	    state.isAuthenticated = false;

=== 全文第 138 页 / 365 页 ===

0064	    state.sessionExpiresAt = '';
0065	  }
0066	
0067	  return { ...toRefs(state), setSession, hydrate, logout, clear };

0001	<script setup lang="ts">
0002	import { ref } from 'vue';
0003	import { useRouter } from 'vue-router';
0004	import { AdminApiError } from '@/services/apiClient';
0005	import {
0006	  FIXED_ADMIN_CAPABILITY,
0007	  FIXED_ADMIN_LOGIN,
0008	  loginAdmin,
0009	} from '@/services/adminAuth';
0010	import { useAdminSessionStore } from '@/stores/adminSession';
0011	
0012	const router = useRouter();
0013	const session = useAdminSessionStore();
0014	const password = ref('');
0015	const isSubmitting = ref(false);
0016	const errorMessage = ref('');
0017	
0018	async function submit(): Promise<void> {
0019	  if (!password.value || isSubmitting.value) return;
0020	  isSubmitting.value = true;
0021	  errorMessage.value = '';
0022	  try {
0023	    session.setSession(await loginAdmin(password.value));
0024	    password.value = '';
0025	    await router.push('/');
0026	  } catch (error) {
0027	    errorMessage.value =

0036	const taskLabels = {
0037	  content_review: '内容审核',

0039	  identity_access: '身份授权',
0040	  followup: '跟进记录',
0041	} as const;
0042	
0043	async function load(): Promise<void> {
0044	  loading.value = true;
0045	  loadError.value = '';
0046	  permissionDenied.value = false;
0047	  conflict.value = false;
0048	  expired.value = false;
0049	  submitError.value = false;
0050	  try {
0051	    task.value = await fetchTaskDetail(
0052	      String(route.params.taskId),
0053	      session.accessToken,
0054	    );
0055	  } catch (error) {
0056	    handleError(error, true);
0057	  } finally {
0058	    loading.value = false;
0059	  }
0060	}
0061	
0062	function handleError(error: unknown, duringLoad = false): void {
0063	  if (!(error instanceof AdminApiError)) {
0064	    if (duringLoad) loadError.value = '暂时没有加载到任务，请重新尝试';
0065	    else submitError.value = true;
0066	    return;
0067	  }
0068	  if (error.code === 'SESSION_EXPIRED' || error.status === 401) {
0069	    expired.value = true;
0070	    task.value = undefined;
0071	    session.clear();
0072	    return;
0073	  }
0074	  if (error.code === 'FORBIDDEN' || error.status === 403) {
0075	    permissionDenied.value = true;
0076	    return;
0077	  }
0078	  if (error.code === 'VERSION_CONFLICT' || error.status === 409) {
0079	    conflict.value = true;
0080	    return;
0081	  }
0082	  if (duringLoad) loadError.value = error.message;
0083	  else submitError.value = true;
0084	}
0085

0090	  );
0091	}
0092	async function release(): Promise<void> {
0093	  if (!task.value) return;
0094	  await mutate(() =>
0095	    releaseTask(task.value!.task_id, task.value!.object_version, session.accessToken),
0096	  );
0097	}
0098	async function submit(decision: TaskDecision): Promise<void> {
0099	  if (!task.value) return;
0100	  submitError.value = false;
0101	  await mutate(() =>
0102	    decideTask(
0103	      task.value!.task_id,
0104	      task.value!.object_version,
0105	      decision,
0106	      session.accessToken,
0107	    ),
0108	  );
0109	}
0110	async function mutate(action: () => Promise<TaskMutationResult>): Promise<void> {
0111	  busy.value = true;
0112	  try {
0113	    await action();
0114	    await load();
0115	  } catch (error) {
0116	    handleError(error);
0117	  } finally {
0118	    busy.value = false;
0119	  }
0120	}
0121	function goLogin(): void {
0122	  void router.push('/login');
0123	}
0124	function goBack(): void {
0125	  void router.push('/');
0126	}
0127	onMounted(() => void load());
0128	</script>
0129	
0130	<template>
0131	  <AdminShell
0132	    :title="task ? `${taskLabels[task.task_kind]} · ${task.task_id}` : '任务详情'"

0046	    request: Request,

0048	    admin_id: str,
0049	    action: str,

0051	) -> None:

0145	async def decide_task(
0146	    request: Request,
0147	    task_id: str,
0148	    body: dict[str, Any],
0149	    authorization: AuthorizationHeader = None,
0150	) -> ApiEnvelope[TaskMutationResult]:
0151	    subject = _admin(request, authorization)
0152	    service = _service(request)
0153	    task = service.tasks.get(task_id)
0154	    if task is None or task.get("is_deleted", False):
0155	        raise ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0156	    model = _decision_model(task["task_kind"], body)
0157	    action_code = getattr(model, "action_code", None)
0158	    if (
0159	        task["task_kind"] == "followup"
0160	        and action_code == "contact_made"
0161	        and service.settings.environment_kind.value == "demo"
0162	    ):
0163	        raise ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED", "演示环境不能记录真实联系结果")
0164	    try:
0165	        data = service.decide(
0166	            task_id,
0167	            object_version=model.object_version,
0168	            admin_id=subject.subject_id,
0169	            request_id=request_id(request),
0170	            idempotency_key=require_idempotency_key(request),
0171	            action=model.action,
0172	            action_code=action_code,
0173	            fact_note=getattr(model, "fact_note", None),
0174	            due_at=getattr(model, "followup_due_at", None) or getattr(model, "next_due_at", None),
0175	            internal_reason=getattr(model, "internal_reason", None),
0176	        )
0177	    except ApiException as error:
0178	        _record_mutation_error(request, task_id, subject.subject_id, model.action, error)
0179	        raise
0180	    return ApiEnvelope.success(request_id(request), data)

0183	def _decision_model(task_kind: str, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
0184	    models: dict[str, type[BaseModel]] = {

0189	    }

0191	    if model_type is None:

0001	"""HTTP request context, authentication dependencies and safe exception mapping."""
0002	
0003	import logging
0004	import re
0005	from dataclasses import dataclass
0006	from typing import cast
0007	from uuid import uuid4
0008	
0009	from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
0010	from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
0011	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
0012	from starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException
0013	
0014	from app.repositories.protocols import (
0015	    RepositoryError,
0016	    RepositoryNotFound,
0017	    RepositoryUnavailable,
0018	    RepositoryVersionConflict,
0019	)
0020	from app.schemas.envelope import ApiEnvelope
0021	from app.schemas.errors import ApiException, status_error
0022	from app.security.tokens import AuthenticatedSubject
0023	from app.services.auth_service import AuthService
0024	
0025	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
0026	REQUEST_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{0,63}$")
0027	
0028	
0029	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0030	class RequestContext:
0031	    request_id: str
0032	
0033	
0034	def create_request_id() -> str:
0035	    return f"req_{uuid4().hex}"
0036	
0037	
0038	def resolve_request_id(value: str | None) -> str:
0039	    if value is None or value == "":
0040	        return create_request_id()
0041	    if not REQUEST_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(value):
0042	        raise ApiException(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "请求编号格式不正确")
0043	    return value
0044	
0045	
0046	def request_id(request: Request) -> str:
0047	    value = getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)
0048	    return value if isinstance(value, str) else create_request_id()
0049	
0050	
0051	def bearer_token(authorization: str | None) -> str:
0052	    if not authorization:

=== 全文第 169 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/api/dependencies.py ===
0053	        raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0054	    scheme, separator, token = authorization.partition(" ")
0055	    if scheme.lower() != "bearer" or not separator or not token.strip():
0056	        raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0057	    return token.strip()
0058	
0059	
0060	def current_subject(request: Request, authorization: str | None) -> AuthenticatedSubject:
0061	    token = bearer_token(authorization)
0062	    service = cast(AuthService, request.app.state.auth_service)
0063	    return service.authenticate(token)
0064	
0065	
0066	def student_subject(request: Request, authorization: str | None) -> AuthenticatedSubject:
0067	    subject = current_subject(request, authorization)
0068	    if subject.subject_type != "student":
0069	        raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0070	    return subject
0071	
0072	
0073	def admin_subject(request: Request, authorization: str | None) -> AuthenticatedSubject:
0074	    subject = current_subject(request, authorization)
0075	    if subject.subject_type != "admin":
0076	        raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0077	    return subject
0078	
0079	
0080	def require_idempotency_key(request: Request) -> str:
0081	    value = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
0082	    if not value or len(value) > 128 or not value.strip():
0083	        raise ApiException(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "缺少有效的幂等键")
0084	    return value.strip()
0085	
0086	
0087	def register_exception_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
0088	    @app.exception_handler(ApiException)
0089	    async def api_exception_handler(request: Request, error: ApiException) -> JSONResponse:
0090	        return _error_response(request, error)
0091	
0092	    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
0093	    async def validation_exception_handler(
0094	        request: Request,
0095	        error: RequestValidationError,
0096	    ) -> JSONResponse:
0097	        del error
0098	        return _error_response(request, ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED"))
0099	
0100	    @app.exception_handler(RepositoryVersionConflict)
0101	    async def repository_version_conflict_handler(
0102	        request: Request,
0103	        error: RepositoryVersionConflict,
0104	    ) -> JSONResponse:
0105	        return _error_response(
0106	            request,
0107	            ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=error.current_version),
0108	        )
0109	
0110	    @app.exception_handler(RepositoryNotFound)
0111	    async def repository_not_found_handler(
0112	        request: Request,
0113	        error: RepositoryNotFound,
0114	    ) -> JSONResponse:
0115	        del error

0119	    async def repository_unavailable_handler(
0120	        request: Request,
0121	        error: RepositoryUnavailable,
0122	    ) -> JSONResponse:

0128	        request: Request,

0133

0135	    async def http_exception_handler(
0136	        request: Request,
0137	        error: StarletteHTTPException,
0138	    ) -> JSONResponse:
0139	        return _error_response(request, status_error(error.status_code))
0140	
0141	    @app.exception_handler(Exception)
0142	    async def unhandled_exception_handler(request: Request, error: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
0143	        logger.error("unhandled backend exception type=%s", type(error).__name__)
0144	        return _error_response(request, ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR"))
0145	
0146	
0147	def _error_response(request: Request, error: ApiException) -> JSONResponse:
0148	    payload: ApiEnvelope[object] = ApiEnvelope.failure(request_id(request), error.to_error())
0149	    return JSONResponse(status_code=error.status_code, content=payload.model_dump(mode="json"))

=== 文件：backend/app/audit/__init__.py ===
0001	"""Minimal audit event writers."""

=== 文件：backend/app/audit/writer.py ===
0001	"""Minimal-fact, append-only audit event writer."""
0002	
0003	from collections.abc import Mapping
0004	from datetime import datetime
0005	from typing import Any
0006	from uuid import uuid4
0007	
0008	from app.repositories.audit_repository import (
0009	    AuditEventRecord,
0010	    AuditRepository,
0011	    InMemoryAuditRepository,
0012	)
0013	
0014	# Only these facts are safe to keep in the first audit projection. Full answers,
0015	# post bodies and identity values are intentionally absent from the allowlist.
0016	SAFE_DETAIL_KEYS = frozenset(
0017	    {
0018	        "action_code",
0019	        "content_hash",
0020	        "error_category",
0021	        "model_version",
0022	        "next_due_at",
0023	        "object_version",
0024	        "outcome_category",
0025	        "prompt_version",
0026	        "requested_fields",
0027	        "resource_version",
0028	        "status",
0029	        "task_kind",
0030	        "task_type",
0031	        "output_status",
0032	        "fallback_reason",
0033	    }
0034	)
0035	
0036	
0037	class AuditWriter:

=== 全文第 178 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/audit/writer.py ===
0038	    def __init__(
0039	        self,
0040	        repository: AuditRepository | None = None,
0041	        *,
0042	        environment_id: str = "unconfigured",
0043	    ) -> None:
0044	        self.repository = repository or InMemoryAuditRepository()
0045	        self.environment_id = environment_id
0046	
0047	    def write(
0048	        self,
0049	        *,
0050	        request_id: str,
0051	        actor_type: str,
0052	        actor_id: str,
0053	        capability: str | None,
0054	        action: str,
0055	        resource_type: str,
0056	        resource_id: str,
0057	        data_scope: str,
0058	        outcome: str,
0059	        reason_code: str | None,
0060	        occurred_at: datetime,
0061	        facts: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
0062	    ) -> AuditEventRecord:
0063	        event = AuditEventRecord(
0064	            event_id=f"audit_{uuid4().hex}",
0065	            request_id=request_id,
0066	            environment_id=self.environment_id,
0067	            actor_type=actor_type,
0068	            actor_id=actor_id,
0069	            capability=capability,
0070	            action=action,
0071	            resource_type=resource_type,
0072	            resource_id=resource_id,
0073	            data_scope=data_scope,
0074	            outcome=outcome,
0075	            reason_code=reason_code,
0076	            occurred_at=occurred_at,
0077	            details=_safe_details(facts or {}),
0078	        )
0079	        return self.repository.append(event)
0080	
0081	
0082	def _safe_details(facts: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
0083	    return {key: _safe_value(value) for key, value in facts.items() if key in SAFE_DETAIL_KEYS}
0084	
0085	
0086	def _safe_value(value: Any) -> Any:
0087	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
0088	        return {key: _safe_value(item) for key, item in value.items() if key in SAFE_DETAIL_KEYS}
0089	    if isinstance(value, list):
0090	        return [_safe_value(item) for item in value]
0091	    if isinstance(value, tuple):
0092	        return [_safe_value(item) for item in value]

=== 全文第 179 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/audit/writer.py ===
0093	    if isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)) or value is None:
0094	        return value
0095	    return str(value)

=== 文件：backend/app/config/ai_prompt.py ===
0001	"""Frozen DeepSeek prompt and protocol constants for the two allowed AI tasks."""  # noqa: E501
0002	
0003	from __future__ import annotations
0004	
0005	DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL = "https://api.deepseek.com"
0006	DEEPSEEK_CHAT_PATH = "/chat/completions"
0007	DEEPSEEK_CHAT_URL = f"{DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL}{DEEPSEEK_CHAT_PATH}"
0008	REQUEST_MODEL = "deepseek-v4-flash"
0009	RESOLVED_MODEL_VERSION = "DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731"
0010	PROMPT_VERSION = "xinyu-v2-system-v1"
0011	DEEPSEEK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 8.0
0012	DEEPSEEK_MAX_CONCURRENCY = 4
0013	
0014	SYSTEM_PROMPT = """你是“心语 V2”的受限文字辅助模块。心语 V2 是面向大学生的心理健康自助观察与匿名轻社区微信小程序，不是医疗诊断、心理治疗、风险预测或危机处置系统。
0015	
0016	你只能执行输入 task_type 指定的一项任务：
0017	1. assessment_explanation：根据已经由固定规则生成的量表分数、固定分层和去身份化派生事实，写简短、平静、非诊断的辅助说明。
0018	2. treehole_review_assist：根据已经脱敏的匿名树洞文本，输出供规则引擎和人工审核参考的结构化建议。
0019	
0020	共同限制：
0021	- 只使用输入中明确给出的事实，不猜测身份、疾病、动机、人格、学校、家庭或现实处境。
0022	- 不进行量表评分，不修改 fixed_band，不判断自伤风险，不输出诊断、治疗方案、危机等级或“高危/低危”。
0023	- 不要求或复原姓名、学号、手机号、地址、账号、联系方式及其他身份信息。
0024	- 不输出思维链、内部推理、模型置信度解释或隐藏规则。
0025	- 不承诺学校、老师、家人或平台会主动联系或完成处置。
0026	- 语气自然、平静、尊重，不训诫、不夸大、不使用空泛的“你一定会好起来”。
0027	- 必须只输出一个合法 JSON 对象，不要输出 Markdown、代码围栏、前后说明或 JSON 之外的文本。
0028	
0029	当 task_type 为 assessment_explanation：
0030	- fixed_summary 是不可改写的事实基线；不得降低或提高固定分层。
0031	- 输出 1 段 40 至 90 个汉字的 summary、0 至 3 条 observations、1 至 3 条 practical_steps 和固定边界提醒。
0032	- practical_steps 只能是低风险、可选择的自我照顾或寻求支持建议；不得给出药物、疗法、诊断或替代现实专业帮助的指令。
0033	- 如果输入缺失、互相矛盾、包含安全确认信息或要求你判断风险，返回 status="needs_fallback"。
0034	
0035	assessment_explanation 的 JSON 结构：
0036	{
0037	  "task_type": "assessment_explanation",
0038	  "status": "ok|needs_fallback",
0039	  "summary": "string",
0040	  "observations": ["string"],
0041	  "practical_steps": ["string"],
0042	  "boundary_notice": "这段说明用于帮助你阅读固定结果，不是诊断或专业评估。"
0043	}
0044	
0045	当 task_type 为 treehole_review_assist：
0046	- 输入文本已经脱敏；不要尝试恢复被遮罩内容。
0047	- 识别内容安全、个人信息残留、社区互动问题和需要人工关注的支持信号，但不要诊断或给出危机等级。
0048	- recommended_route 只能是 allow、protect、manual_review、safety_review 之一。回应不允许使用 protect，应改为 manual_review。
0049	- evidence_spans 最多 3 条，每条只能引用完成判断所需的最短片段；不得输出全文或被遮罩信息。
0050	- 无法确定、文本过短、结构异常或规则冲突时使用 manual_review。
0051	
0052	treehole_review_assist 的 JSON 结构：
0053	{
0054	  "task_type": "treehole_review_assist",
0055	  "status": "ok|needs_fallback",
0056	  "content_safety": "clear|possible_issue|clear_issue|unknown",
0057	  "wellbeing_signal": "none|supportive_attention|manual_attention|unknown",

=== 全文第 180 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/config/ai_prompt.py ===
0058	  "privacy_signal": "clear|possible_residual|unknown",
0059	  "community_issue": ["harassment|sexual_content|violence|illegal_content|spam|none|unknown"],

0061	  "recommended_route": "allow|protect|manual_review|safety_review",

=== 文件：backend/app/config/deployment.py ===
0001	"""Validation helpers for CloudBase deployment-time configuration.
0002	
0003	The validator intentionally accepts mappings (rather than reading the process
0004	environment) so CI and deployment operators can validate a rendered template
0005	without ever printing secret values.
0006	"""
0007	
0008	from dataclasses import dataclass
0009	from urllib.parse import urlparse
0010	
0011	
0012	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0013	class DeploymentValidation:
0014	    """Safe validation result; values and secrets are never included."""
0015	
0016	    errors: tuple[str, ...]
0017	
0018	    @property
0019	    def valid(self) -> bool:
0020	        return not self.errors
0021	
0022	
0023	_REQUIRED = (
0024	    "CLOUDBASE_ENV_ID_DEMO",
0025	    "CLOUDBASE_ENV_ID_AUTHORIZED",
0026	    "DEMO_NAMESPACE",
0027	    "AUTHORIZED_NAMESPACE",
0028	    "ADMIN_WEB_ORIGIN_DEMO",
0029	    "ADMIN_WEB_ORIGIN_AUTHORIZED",
0030	)
0031	
0032	
0033	def validate_deployment_config(values: dict[str, str]) -> DeploymentValidation:
0034	    """Check environment isolation and HTTPS origins without exposing secrets.
0035	
0036	    Empty values and ``${...}`` placeholders are reported as incomplete, while
0037	    all other values are treated as operator-provided and are never echoed.
0038	    """
0039	
0040	    errors: list[str] = []
0041	    normalized = {key: (value or "").strip() for key, value in values.items()}
0042	    for key in _REQUIRED:
0043	        value = normalized.get(key, "")
0044	        if not value or (value.startswith("${") and value.endswith("}")):
0045	            errors.append(f"{key} is not configured")
0046	
0047	    demo_env = normalized.get("CLOUDBASE_ENV_ID_DEMO", "")
0048	    authorized_env = normalized.get("CLOUDBASE_ENV_ID_AUTHORIZED", "")
0049	    if demo_env and authorized_env and demo_env == authorized_env:
0050	        errors.append("CloudBase environment IDs must differ")
0051	
0052	    demo_namespace = normalized.get("DEMO_NAMESPACE", "")
0053	    authorized_namespace = normalized.get("AUTHORIZED_NAMESPACE", "")
0054	    if demo_namespace and authorized_namespace and demo_namespace == authorized_namespace:
0055	        errors.append("database namespaces must differ")
0056	
0057	    for key in ("ADMIN_WEB_ORIGIN_DEMO", "ADMIN_WEB_ORIGIN_AUTHORIZED"):
0058	        origin = normalized.get(key, "")
0059	        if origin and not (origin.startswith("${") and origin.endswith("}")):
0060	            parsed = urlparse(origin)
0061	            if parsed.scheme != "https" or not parsed.netloc or parsed.path not in ("", "/"):
0062	                errors.append(f"{key} must be an HTTPS origin without a path")
0063	
0064	    return DeploymentValidation(tuple(dict.fromkeys(errors)))

=== 文件：backend/app/config/environments.py ===
0001	"""Environment classification and isolation checks for the backend."""
0002	
0003	from collections.abc import Iterable
0004	from dataclasses import dataclass
0005	from enum import StrEnum
0006	
0007	
0008	class EnvironmentKind(StrEnum):
0009	    DEMO = "demo"

=== 全文第 181 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/config/environments.py ===
0010	    AUTHORIZED = "authorized"
0011	    UNCONFIGURED = "unconfigured"
0012	
0013	
0014	class ConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
0015	    """Base error for an unsafe or incomplete deployment configuration."""
0016	
0017	
0018	class EnvironmentMismatchError(ConfigurationError):
0019	    """Raised when a known environment is paired with the wrong mode."""
0020	
0021	
0022	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0023	class EnvironmentDecision:
0024	    """The result of comparing the declared mode and the environment registry."""
0025	
0026	    declared_kind: EnvironmentKind
0027	    matched_kind: EnvironmentKind
0028	
0029	
0030	def parse_demo_mode(value: str | None) -> bool | None:
0031	    """Parse the explicit deployment switch without accepting ambiguous values."""
0032	
0033	    if value is None or not value.strip():
0034	        return None
0035	    normalized = value.strip().lower()
0036	    if normalized in {"true", "1"}:
0037	        return True
0038	    if normalized in {"false", "0"}:
0039	        return False
0040	    raise ConfigurationError("DEMO_MODE must be true or false")
0041	
0042	
0043	def resolve_environment(
0044	    *,
0045	    demo_mode: bool | None,
0046	    cloudbase_env_id: str | None,
0047	    demo_env_ids: Iterable[str] = (),
0048	    authorized_env_ids: Iterable[str] = (),
0049	) -> EnvironmentDecision:
0050	    """Resolve a mode only when the CloudBase environment is explicitly registered.
0051	
0052	    An unknown or incomplete registry never becomes a usable environment. A known
0053	    opposite-mode pairing raises so a real environment cannot be disguised as demo.
0054	    """
0055	
0056	    demo_ids = frozenset(item.strip() for item in demo_env_ids if item.strip())
0057	    authorized_ids = frozenset(item.strip() for item in authorized_env_ids if item.strip())
0058	    if demo_ids & authorized_ids:
0059	        raise ConfigurationError("CloudBase environment IDs cannot overlap between modes")
0060	
0061	    declared_kind = (
0062	        EnvironmentKind.DEMO
0063	        if demo_mode is True
0064	        else EnvironmentKind.AUTHORIZED
0065	        if demo_mode is False
0066	        else EnvironmentKind.UNCONFIGURED
0067	    )
0068	    if not cloudbase_env_id or demo_mode is None:
0069	        return EnvironmentDecision(declared_kind, EnvironmentKind.UNCONFIGURED)
0070	
0071	    normalized_env_id = cloudbase_env_id.strip()
0072	    if demo_mode is True:
0073	        if normalized_env_id in authorized_ids:
0074	            raise EnvironmentMismatchError(
0075	                "DEMO_MODE cannot target an authorized CloudBase environment"
0076	            )
0077	        matched_kind = (
0078	            EnvironmentKind.DEMO if normalized_env_id in demo_ids else EnvironmentKind.UNCONFIGURED
0079	        )
0080	    else:
0081	        if normalized_env_id in demo_ids:
0082	            raise EnvironmentMismatchError(
0083	                "authorized mode cannot target a demo CloudBase environment"

=== 全文第 182 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/config/environments.py ===
0084	            )
0085	        matched_kind = (
0086	            EnvironmentKind.AUTHORIZED
0087	            if normalized_env_id in authorized_ids
0088	            else EnvironmentKind.UNCONFIGURED
0089	        )
0090	    return EnvironmentDecision(declared_kind, matched_kind)

0059	def project_assessment_input(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:

0168	        }:

0170	        if output.get("privacy_signal") not in {"clear", "possible_residual", "unknown"}:
0171	            raise PolicyViolation("隐私信号枚举不正确")
0172	        issues = output.get("community_issue")
0173	        if not isinstance(issues, list) or not 1 <= len(issues) <= 6:
0174	            raise PolicyViolation("社区问题字段不正确")
0175	        issue_values = {
0176	            "harassment",
0177	            "sexual_content",
0178	            "violence",
0179	            "illegal_content",
0180	            "spam",
0181	            "none",
0182	            "unknown",
0183	        }
0184	        if any(item not in issue_values for item in issues):
0185	            raise PolicyViolation("社区问题枚举不正确")
0186	        spans = output.get("evidence_spans")
0187	        if (
0188	            not isinstance(spans, list)
0189	            or len(spans) > 3
0190	            or any(not isinstance(item, str) or len(item) > 120 for item in spans)
0191	        ):
0192	            raise PolicyViolation("证据片段字段不正确")
0193	        if source_text is None:
0194	            raise PolicyViolation("缺少脱敏原文用于证据校验")

=== 全文第 188 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/domain/ai_policy.py ===
0195	        if any(span not in source_text or "［已隐藏" in span for span in spans):
0196	            raise PolicyViolation("证据片段必须来自脱敏文本")
0197	        route = output.get("recommended_route")
0198	        if route not in {"allow", "protect", "manual_review", "safety_review"}:
0199	            raise PolicyViolation("推荐路径枚举不正确")
0200	        if content_type == "response" and route == "protect":
0201	            raise PolicyViolation("回应不得推荐保护展示")
0202	        _require_string(output, "review_note", 240)
0203	        _reject_sensitive_text(output["review_note"])
0204	        _reject_prohibited_text(output["review_note"])
0205	    return dict(output)
0206	
0207	
0208	def _require_task(data: Mapping[str, Any], task_type: str) -> None:
0209	    if data.get("task_type") != task_type:
0210	        raise PolicyViolation("AI 任务类型不在允许范围")
0211	
0212	
0213	def _reject_unknown(data: Mapping[str, Any], allowed: set[str]) -> None:
0214	    unknown = set(data) - allowed
0215	    if unknown:
0216	        raise PolicyViolation("AI 输入或输出包含未允许字段")
0217	
0218	
0219	def _require_string(data: Mapping[str, Any], key: str, maximum: int) -> str:
0220	    value = data.get(key)
0221	    if not isinstance(value, str) or len(value) > maximum:
0222	        raise PolicyViolation(f"字段 {key} 格式不正确")
0223	    return value
0224	
0225	
0226	def _require_string_list(
0227	    data: Mapping[str, Any], key: str, *, minimum: int = 0, maximum: int, item_max: int
0228	) -> None:
0229	    value = data.get(key)
0230	    if not isinstance(value, list) or not minimum <= len(value) <= maximum:
0231	        raise PolicyViolation(f"字段 {key} 格式不正确")
0232	    if any(not isinstance(item, str) or len(item) > item_max for item in value):
0233	        raise PolicyViolation(f"字段 {key} 格式不正确")
0234	
0235	
0236	def _reject_sensitive_text(value: str) -> None:
0237	    if _PHONE_PATTERN.search(value) or any(
0238	        marker.lower() in value.lower() for marker in _IDENTITY_MARKERS
0239	    ):
0240	        raise PolicyViolation("文本包含身份或联系方式")
0241	
0242	
0243	def _reject_prohibited_text(value: str) -> None:
0244	    if any(marker in value for marker in _AI_PROHIBITED):
0245	        raise PolicyViolation("AI 输出包含受限内容")
0246	
0247	
0248	def _copy_json_fields(data: Mapping[str, Any], fields: set[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
0249	    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
0250	    for key in fields:
0251	        value = data[key]
0252	        result[key] = list(value) if isinstance(value, list) else value
0253	    return result
0254	
0255	
0256	def ai_metadata() -> dict[str, str]:
0257	    return {
0258	        "request_model": REQUEST_MODEL,
0259	        "resolved_model_version": RESOLVED_MODEL_VERSION,
0260	        "prompt_version": PROMPT_VERSION,

0085	def questionnaire_catalog(module_code: ModuleCode) -> CatalogQuestionnaire:
0086	    if module_code == "phq9":
0087	        return _phq9_catalog()
0088	    if module_code == "gad7":
0089	        return _gad7_catalog()
0090	    if module_code == "sleep_observation":
0091	        return _sleep_catalog()
0092	    raise AssessmentValidationError()

0094	
0095	def score_questionnaire(
0096	    questionnaire_or_module_code: ModuleCode | AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument,
0097	    answers: list[tuple[str, str]],
0098	) -> ScoreOutcome:
0099	    if isinstance(questionnaire_or_module_code, AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument):
0100	        module_code = questionnaire_or_module_code.module_code
0101	        questions = questionnaire_questions(questionnaire_or_module_code)
0102	    else:
0103	        module_code = questionnaire_or_module_code
0104	        questions = questionnaire_catalog(module_code).questions
0105	    validated = _validate_answers(questions, answers)
0106	    if module_code == "phq9":
0107	        return _score_phq9(validated)
0108	    if module_code == "gad7":
0109	        return _score_gad7(validated)
0110	    return _score_sleep(validated)
0111

0113	def questionnaire_questions(
0114	    questionnaire: AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument,
0115	) -> tuple[CatalogQuestion, ...]:
0116	    """Validate a published questionnaire and expose its private scoring view."""
0117	
0118	    if questionnaire.non_diagnostic_copy_version != NON_DIAGNOSTIC_COPY_VERSION:
0119	        raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0120	    if questionnaire.score_rule.get("scoring_rule_version") != SCORING_RULE_VERSION:
0121	        raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0122	
0123	    expected_keys: tuple[str, ...]
0124	    if questionnaire.module_code == "phq9":
0125	        expected_keys = (*tuple(f"q{number}" for number in range(1, 10)), "impact")

=== 文件：backend/app/domain/assessment_rules.py ===
0126	        if questionnaire.score_rule.get("impact_question_key") != "impact":
0127	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0128	        if questionnaire.score_rule.get("safety_question_key") != PHQ9_Q9_KEY:
0129	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0130	    elif questionnaire.module_code == "gad7":
0131	        expected_keys = tuple(f"q{number}" for number in range(1, 8))
0132	    else:
0133	        expected_keys = tuple(f"q{number}" for number in range(1, 9))
0134	        if questionnaire.score_rule.get("dimensions") != [
0135	            "rhythm",
0136	            "duration_fact",
0137	            "bedtime_resistance",
0138	            "bedtime_reasons",
0139	            "recovery_daytime_impact",
0140	        ]:
0141	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0142	
0143	    if len(questionnaire.questions) != len(expected_keys):
0144	        raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0145	    if tuple(question.question_key for question in questionnaire.questions) != expected_keys:
0146	        raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0147	    if tuple(question.order for question in questionnaire.questions) != tuple(
0148	        range(1, len(expected_keys) + 1)
0149	    ):
0150	        raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0151	
0152	    questions: list[CatalogQuestion] = []
0153	    for question in questionnaire.questions:
0154	        if not question.options:
0155	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0156	        if len({option.option_key for option in question.options}) != len(question.options):
0157	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0158	        if any(option.score < 0 for option in question.options):
0159	            raise AssessmentValidationError("UNSUPPORTED_QUESTIONNAIRE")
0160	        questions.append(
0161	            CatalogQuestion(
0162	                question_key=question.question_key,
0163	                order=question.order,
0164	                text=question.text,
0165	                options=tuple(question.options),
0166	                required=not (
0167	                    questionnaire.module_code == "sleep_observation"
0168	                    and question.question_key == "q8"
0169	                ),
0170	                multiple=(
0171	                    questionnaire.module_code == "sleep_observation"
0172	                    and question.question_key == "q8"
0173	                ),
0174	            )
0175	        )
0176	    return tuple(questions)

0464	def _validate_answers(
0465	    questions: tuple[CatalogQuestion, ...],
0466	    answers: list[tuple[str, str]],
0467	) -> list[tuple[CatalogQuestion, list[QuestionOptionModel]]]:
0468	    grouped: dict[str, list[QuestionOptionModel]] = {}
0469	    order_seen: list[str] = []
0470	    current_index = 0
0471	    for question_key, option_key in answers:
0472	        if current_index >= len(questions):
0473	            raise AssessmentValidationError()
0474	        while (
0475	            current_index < len(questions) and questions[current_index].question_key != question_key
0476	        ):
0477	            if questions[current_index].required:
0478	                raise AssessmentValidationError()
0479	            current_index += 1
0480	        if current_index >= len(questions):
0481	            raise AssessmentValidationError()
0482	        question = questions[current_index]
0483	        if not question.multiple and question_key in grouped:
0484	            raise AssessmentValidationError()
0485	        option = next((item for item in question.options if item.option_key == option_key), None)
0486	        if option is None:
0487	            raise AssessmentValidationError()

=== 全文第 198 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/domain/assessment_rules.py ===
0488	        if question.multiple:
0489	            if option in grouped.get(question_key, []):
0490	                raise AssessmentValidationError()
0491	        grouped.setdefault(question_key, []).append(option)
0492	        if not order_seen or order_seen[-1] != question_key:
0493	            order_seen.append(question_key)
0494	        if not question.multiple:
0495	            current_index += 1
0496	
0497	    for question in questions:
0498	        if question.required and question.question_key not in grouped:
0499	            raise AssessmentValidationError()
0500	    extra_keys = {
0501	        key for key in grouped if key not in {question.question_key for question in questions}
0502	    }
0503	    if extra_keys:
0504	        raise AssessmentValidationError()
0505	
0506	    q8_answers = grouped.get("q8", [])
0507	    if q8_answers and any(item.option_key == SLEEP_Q8_REFUSAL_OPTION_KEY for item in q8_answers):
0508	        if len(q8_answers) != 1:
0509	            raise AssessmentValidationError()
0510	
0511	    return [(question, grouped.get(question.question_key, [])) for question in questions]

0541	        result_state="higher_score" if score >= 10 else "ordinary",

0642	def _phq9_band(score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
0643	    if score <= 4:
0644	        return "0-4", "这次记录里，近期情绪相关信号较少。"
0645	    if score <= 9:

0647	    if score <= 14:
0648	        return "10-14", "这次记录显示，你近期的情绪困扰比较明显。"
0649	    if score <= 19:
0650	        return "15-19", "这次记录显示，你近期的情绪困扰较重。"

0654	def _gad7_band(score: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
0655	    if score <= 4:

0153	    @model_validator(mode="after")
0154	    def validate_state_payload(self) -> AssessmentSessionDocument:
0155	        if self.state == "completed":
0156	            if self.safety_triggered and self.safety_confirmation_state == "uncertain":
0157	                if self.answers is not None or self.answered_count is None:
0158	                    raise ValueError(
0159	                        "uncertain safety support sessions persist answered_count but not answers"
0160	                    )
0161	            else:
0162	                if self.answers is None or self.answered_count is None:
0163	                    raise ValueError(
0164	                        "completed sessions must include final answers and answered_count"
0165	                    )
0166	                if self.answered_count != len(self.answers):
0167	                    raise ValueError("answered_count must match the number of final answers")
0168	            if self.completed_at is None:
0169	                raise ValueError("completed sessions must include completed_at")
0170	            if self.abandoned_at is not None:
0171	                raise ValueError("completed sessions cannot include abandoned_at")
0172	            return self
0173	
0174	        if self.state == "in_progress":
0175	            if self.completed_at is not None or self.abandoned_at is not None:
0176	                raise ValueError("in_progress sessions cannot include completed_at or abandoned_at")

=== 文件：backend/app/domain/models.py ===
0177	        elif self.state == "abandoned":
0178	            if self.abandoned_at is None:
0179	                raise ValueError("abandoned sessions must include abandoned_at")
0180	            if self.completed_at is not None:
0181	                raise ValueError("abandoned sessions cannot include completed_at")
0182	        elif self.state == "expired":
0183	            if self.completed_at is not None or self.abandoned_at is not None:
0184	                raise ValueError("expired sessions cannot include completed_at or abandoned_at")

0191	class AssessmentResultDocument(DocumentModel):
0192	    session_id: NonEmptyString
0193	    user_id: NonEmptyString
0194	    module_code: Literal["phq9", "gad7", "sleep_observation"]
0195	    scoring_rule_version: NonEmptyString
0196	    answers_snapshot: list[AssessmentAnswerModel] | None = None
0197	    fixed_summary: NonEmptyString
0198	    reference_band: str | None = None
0199	    boundary_notice: NonEmptyString
0200	    ai_assist_snapshot_id: str | None = None
0201	    result_state: Literal["ordinary", "higher_score", "safety_support"]
0202	    score: int | None = None
0203	    dimension_summary: dict[str, Any]
0204	    safety_state: Literal["not_triggered", "can_be_safe", "uncertain", "cannot_be_safe"]
0205	    visible_copy_version: NonEmptyString
0206	    deleted_at: datetime | None = None
0207	
0208	    @model_validator(mode="after")
0209	    def validate_result_projection(self) -> AssessmentResultDocument:
0210	        if self.safety_state == "cannot_be_safe":
0211	            raise ValueError("cannot_be_safe assessments must not persist any result")
0212	
0213	        if self.result_state == "safety_support":
0214	            if self.answers_snapshot is not None:
0215	                raise ValueError("safety_support results cannot persist answers_snapshot")
0216	            if self.score is not None:
0217	                raise ValueError("safety_support results cannot persist score")
0218	            if self.ai_assist_snapshot_id is not None:
0219	                raise ValueError("safety_support results cannot reference ai_assist_snapshot_id")
0220	            if self.reference_band is not None:
0221	                raise ValueError("safety_support results cannot persist reference_band")
0222	            if self.safety_state != "uncertain":
0223	                raise ValueError("safety_support results must use safety_state uncertain")
0224	            return self

0001	"""Deterministic safety branch rules for PHQ-9 question 9."""
0002	
0003	from __future__ import annotations
0004	
0005	from dataclasses import dataclass

0007	
0008	SafetyConfirmationState = Literal["can_be_safe", "uncertain", "cannot_be_safe"]
0009	SafetyNextStep = Literal["continue_assessment", "show_support_resources", "support_only"]
0010	
0011	SAFETY_CONFIRMATION_QUESTION = (
0012	    "刚才的回答涉及过去两周的感受。我想再确认一下你此刻的状态：现在，你能保证自己是安全的吗？"
0013	)
0014	SAFETY_CONFIRMATION_OPTIONS = {
0015	    "can_be_safe": "我现在可以保证安全",
0016	    "uncertain": "我不太确定",
0017	    "cannot_be_safe": "我现在无法保证安全",
0018	}
0019	SAFETY_CONFIRMATION_SOURCE = "phq9_current_safety_confirmation"
0020

0025	    support_required: bool
0026	    creates_immediate_task: bool
0027	
0028	
0029	def decide_safety_branch(state: SafetyConfirmationState) -> SafetyBranchDecision:
0030	    if state == "can_be_safe":

=== 全文第 210 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/domain/safety_rules.py ===
0031	        return SafetyBranchDecision(
0032	            next_step="continue_assessment",

=== 文件：backend/app/integrations/deepseek_client.py ===
0001	"""Small, fixed-contract DeepSeek HTTP adapter.
0002	
0003	The adapter has no business policy: callers must project and validate fields using
0004	``app.domain.ai_policy`` before and after invoking it.
0005	"""
0006	
0007	from __future__ import annotations
0008	
0009	import asyncio
0010	import json
0011	from collections.abc import Mapping
0012	from typing import Any
0013	
0014	import httpx
0015	
0016	from app.config.ai_prompt import (
0017	    DEEPSEEK_CHAT_URL,
0018	    DEEPSEEK_MAX_CONCURRENCY,
0019	    DEEPSEEK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
0020	    REQUEST_MODEL,
0021	    SYSTEM_PROMPT,
0022	)
0023	
0024	
0025	class DeepSeekUnavailable(RuntimeError):
0026	    """Raised when DeepSeek cannot produce a usable JSON response."""
0027	
0028	
0029	class DeepSeekClient:
0030	    def __init__(
0031	        self,
0032	        *,
0033	        api_key: str | None,
0034	        endpoint: str = DEEPSEEK_CHAT_URL,

=== 全文第 211 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/integrations/deepseek_client.py ===
0035	        transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
0036	        timeout: float = DEEPSEEK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
0037	    ) -> None:
0038	        self._api_key = api_key.strip() if api_key else None
0039	        self._endpoint = endpoint
0040	        self._transport = transport
0041	        self._timeout = timeout
0042	        self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(DEEPSEEK_MAX_CONCURRENCY)
0043	
0044	    async def complete(self, *, task_type: str, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
0045	        if not self._api_key:
0046	            raise DeepSeekUnavailable("DeepSeek 未配置")
0047	        request_body = {
0048	            "model": REQUEST_MODEL,
0049	            "messages": [
0050	                {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
0051	                {"role": "user", "content": json.dumps(dict(payload), ensure_ascii=False)},
0052	            ],
0053	            "temperature": 0.2,
0054	            "response_format": {"type": "json_object"},
0055	            "stream": False,
0056	        }
0057	        try:
0058	            async with self._semaphore:
0059	                async with httpx.AsyncClient(
0060	                    timeout=self._timeout,
0061	                    transport=self._transport,
0062	                ) as client:
0063	                    response = await client.post(
0064	                        self._endpoint,
0065	                        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self._api_key}"},
0066	                        json=request_body,
0067	                    )
0068	                    response.raise_for_status()
0069	                    body = response.json()
0070	        except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError, TypeError) as error:
0071	            raise DeepSeekUnavailable("DeepSeek 请求失败") from error
0072	        content = _extract_content(body)
0073	        if not content:
0074	            raise DeepSeekUnavailable("DeepSeek 返回为空")
0075	        try:
0076	            parsed = json.loads(content)
0077	        except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
0078	            raise DeepSeekUnavailable("DeepSeek 返回不是合法 JSON") from error
0079	        if not isinstance(parsed, dict) or parsed.get("task_type") != task_type:
0080	            raise DeepSeekUnavailable("DeepSeek 返回结构不匹配")
0081	        return parsed
0082	
0083	
0084	def _extract_content(body: Any) -> str | None:
0085	    if not isinstance(body, dict):
0086	        return None
0087	    choices = body.get("choices")

=== 全文第 212 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/integrations/deepseek_client.py ===
0088	    if not isinstance(choices, list) or not choices:
0089	        return None
0090	    first = choices[0]
0091	    if not isinstance(first, dict):
0092	        return None
0093	    message = first.get("message")
0094	    if not isinstance(message, dict):
0095	        return None
0096	    content = message.get("content")
0097	    return content if isinstance(content, str) else None

0001	"""School identity adapter boundary for the identity domain."""
0002	
0003	from __future__ import annotations

0010

0012	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0013	class SchoolIdentityVerificationResult:
0014	    status: Literal["verified", "failed", "unavailable"]
0015	    provider_reference: str | None = None
0016	    failed_reason_code: str | None = None
0017	
0018	
0019	class SchoolIdentityProvider(Protocol):
0020	    async def verify_student(
0021	        self,
0022	        *,
0023	        student_name: str,
0024	        student_number: str,
0025	    ) -> SchoolIdentityVerificationResult: ...
0026	
0027	
0028	class UnavailableSchoolIdentityProvider:
0029	    async def verify_student(
0030	        self,
0031	        *,
0032	        student_name: str,
0033	        student_number: str,
0034	    ) -> SchoolIdentityVerificationResult:
0035	        del student_name, student_number
0036	        return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")
0037	
0038	
0039	class HttpSchoolIdentityProvider:
0040	    def __init__(
0041	        self,
0042	        *,
0043	        provider_url: str | None,
0044	        timeout: float = 5.0,
0045	        transport: AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
0046	    ) -> None:
0047	        self._provider_url = provider_url
0048	        self._timeout = timeout
0049	        self._transport = transport
0050	
0051	    async def verify_student(
0052	        self,
0053	        *,

=== 全文第 213 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/integrations/school_identity.py ===
0054	        student_name: str,
0055	        student_number: str,
0056	    ) -> SchoolIdentityVerificationResult:
0057	        if not self._provider_url:
0058	            return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")
0059	        try:
0060	            async with httpx.AsyncClient(
0061	                timeout=self._timeout,
0062	                transport=self._transport,
0063	            ) as client:
0064	                response = await client.post(
0065	                    self._provider_url,
0066	                    json={
0067	                        "student_name": student_name,
0068	                        "student_number": student_number,
0069	                    },
0070	                )
0071	                response.raise_for_status()
0072	            body = response.json()
0073	            if not isinstance(body, dict):
0074	                return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")
0075	            status = body.get("status")
0076	            if status == "verified":
0077	                provider_reference = body.get("provider_reference")
0078	                if not isinstance(provider_reference, str) or not provider_reference:
0079	                    return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")
0080	                return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(
0081	                    status="verified",
0082	                    provider_reference=provider_reference,
0083	                )
0084	            if status == "failed":
0085	                failed_reason_code = body.get("failed_reason_code")
0086	                normalized_reason = (
0087	                    failed_reason_code
0088	                    if isinstance(failed_reason_code, str) and failed_reason_code
0089	                    else None
0090	                )
0091	                return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(
0092	                    status="failed",
0093	                    failed_reason_code=normalized_reason,
0094	                )
0095	            return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")
0096	        except Exception:
0097	            return SchoolIdentityVerificationResult(status="unavailable")

0009

=== 文件：backend/app/integrations/wechat_auth.py ===
0013	    subject_id: str
0014	
0015	
0016	class WechatAuthClient:
0017	    def __init__(
0018	        self,
0019	        *,
0020	        appid: str | None,
0021	        appsecret: str | None,
0022	        endpoint: str = "https://api.weixin.qq.com/sns/jscode2session",
0023	        transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
0024	        timeout: float = 8.0,
0025	    ) -> None:
0026	        self.appid = appid
0027	        self.appsecret = appsecret
0028	        self.endpoint = endpoint
0029	        self.transport = transport
0030	        self.timeout = timeout
0031	
0032	    async def exchange_code(self, code: str) -> WechatIdentity:
0033	        if not self.appid or not self.appsecret:
0034	            raise ApiException(503, "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", "微信登录暂未配置")
0035	        if not code.strip():
0036	            raise ApiException(400, "INVALID_REQUEST")
0037	        params = {
0038	            "appid": self.appid,
0039	            "secret": self.appsecret,
0040	            "js_code": code,
0041	            "grant_type": "authorization_code",
0042	        }
0043	        try:
0044	            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout, transport=self.transport) as client:
0045	                response = await client.get(self.endpoint, params=params)
0046	                response.raise_for_status()
0047	                body = response.json()
0048	        except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError) as error:
0049	            raise ApiException(503, "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", "微信登录服务暂时不可用") from error
0050	        if not isinstance(body, dict):
0051	            raise ApiException(503, "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", "微信登录服务暂时不可用")
0052	        if body.get("errcode") not in (None, 0):
0053	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED", "微信登录凭证无效或已过期")
0054	        openid = body.get("openid")
0055	        unionid = body.get("unionid")
0056	        if not isinstance(openid, str) or not openid:
0057	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED", "微信登录凭证无效或已过期")
0058	        identity_key = unionid if isinstance(unionid, str) and unionid else openid
0059	        subject_id = hashlib.sha256(f"{self.appid}:{identity_key}".encode()).hexdigest()
0060	        return WechatIdentity(subject_id=subject_id)

0010	class AuditEventRecord:
0011	    event_id: str
0012	    request_id: str
0013	    environment_id: str
0014	    actor_type: str
0015	    actor_id: str
0016	    capability: str | None
0017	    action: str
0018	    resource_type: str
0019	    resource_id: str
0020	    data_scope: str
0021	    outcome: str
0022	    reason_code: str | None
0023	    occurred_at: datetime
0024	    details: dict[str, Any]

0026	
0027	
0028	class AuditRepository(Protocol):
0029	    def append(self, event: AuditEventRecord) -> AuditEventRecord: ...
0030	
0031	    def list(self) -> tuple[AuditEventRecord, ...]: ...
0032	
0033	
0034	class InMemoryAuditRepository:
0035	    def __init__(self) -> None:
0036	        self._events: list[AuditEventRecord] = []
0037	        self._lock = RLock()
0038	
0039	    def append(self, event: AuditEventRecord) -> AuditEventRecord:
0040	        with self._lock:
0041	            self._events.append(event)
0042	        return event
0043	
0044	    def list(self) -> tuple[AuditEventRecord, ...]:
0045	        with self._lock:
0046	            return tuple(self._events)
