0039	
0040	    def __init__(
0041	        self,
0042	        *,
0043	        environment_id: str,
0044	        api_key: str,
0045	        base_url: str = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
0046	        session_token: str | None = None,
0047	        transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None,
0048	        timeout: float = 8.0,
0049	    ) -> None:
0050	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
0051	        self.environment_id = environment_id
0052	        self.api_key = api_key
0053	        self.session_token = session_token
0054	        self.transport = transport
0055	        self.timeout = timeout
0056	
0057	    async def query(
0058	        self,
0059	        collection: str,
0060	        where: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
0061	        *,
0062	        cursor: str | None = None,
0063	        limit: int = 20,
0064	    ) -> DocumentPage:
0065	        page_size = max(1, min(limit, 100))
0066	        offset = _decode_cursor(cursor)
0067	        payload = await self._request(
0068	            "find",
0069	            collection,
0070	            params={
0071	                "limit": str(page_size),
0072	                "skip": str(offset),
0073	                "fields": "{}",
0074	                "sort": '{"_id":1}',
0075	            },
0076	            body={"query": _ejson(where or {})},
0077	        )
0078	        data = self._data(payload)
0079	        raw_documents = data.get("list", [])
0080	        if not isinstance(raw_documents, list):
0081	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned an invalid query list")
0082	        documents = tuple(_decode_document(item) for item in raw_documents)
0083	        response_limit = data.get("limit", page_size)
0084	        if not isinstance(response_limit, int) or response_limit < 1:
0085	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned an invalid query limit")
0086	        next_cursor = (

=== 全文第 217 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/cloudbase_gateway.py ===
0087	            _encode_cursor(offset + len(documents)) if len(documents) >= response_limit else None
0088	        )
0089	        return DocumentPage(documents, next_cursor)

0091	    async def conditional_update(
0092	        self,
0093	        collection: str,
0094	        document_id: str,
0095	        *,
0096	        expected_version: int,
0097	        updates: Mapping[str, Any],
0098	    ) -> JsonDocument:
0099	        if "_id" in updates or "version" in updates:
0100	            raise ValueError("_id and version are managed by the repository")
0101	        payload = await self._request(
0102	            "updateOne",
0103	            collection,
0104	            body={
0105	                "query": _ejson({"_id": document_id, "version": expected_version}),
0106	                "data": _ejson({"$set": dict(updates), "$inc": {"version": 1}}),
0107	            },
0108	        )
0109	        await self._require_update_match(payload, collection, document_id, expected_version)
0110	        return await self._find_by_id(collection, document_id)
0111	
0112	    async def logical_delete(
0113	        self,
0114	        collection: str,
0115	        document_id: str,
0116	        *,
0117	        expected_version: int,
0118	    ) -> JsonDocument:
0119	        deleted_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
0120	        payload = await self._request(
0121	            "updateOne",
0122	            collection,
0123	            body={
0124	                "query": _ejson({"_id": document_id, "version": expected_version}),
0125	                "data": _ejson(
0126	                    {
0127	                        "$set": {"is_deleted": True, "deleted_at": deleted_at},
0128	                        "$inc": {"version": 1},
0129	                    }
0130	                ),
0131	            },
0132	        )
0133	        await self._require_update_match(payload, collection, document_id, expected_version)
0134	        return await self._find_by_id(collection, document_id)

0137	        page = await self.query(collection, {"_id": document_id}, limit=1)
0138	        if not page.items:
0139	            raise RepositoryNotFound("CloudBase document not found")

0142	    async def _request(
0143	        self,
0144	        operation: str,
0145	        collection: str,
0146	        *,
0147	        params: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
0148	        body: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
0149	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
0150	        url = (
0151	            f"{self.base_url}/api/v2/envs/{self.environment_id}/databases/"
0152	            f"{collection}/documents:{operation}"
0153	        )
0154	        headers = {
0155	            "X-CloudBase-Authorization": self.api_key,
0156	            "X-CloudBase-TimeStamp": str(int(datetime.now(UTC).timestamp())),
0157	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
0158	        }
0159	        if self.session_token:
0160	            headers["X-CloudBase-SessionToken"] = self.session_token
0161	        try:
0162	            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout, transport=self.transport) as client:
0163	                response = await client.post(url, params=params, headers=headers, json=body or {})
0164	        except httpx.HTTPError as error:
0165	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase HTTP request failed") from error
0166	
0167	        if response.status_code == 404:
0168	            raise RepositoryNotFound("CloudBase document not found")
0169	        if response.status_code == 409:
0170	            raise RepositoryVersionConflict(_current_version(response))
0171	        if response.status_code >= 400:
0172	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned a dependency error")
0173	        try:
0174	            payload = response.json()
0175	        except ValueError as error:
0176	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned invalid JSON") from error
0177	        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
0178	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned an invalid response")
0179	        response_code = payload.get("code")
0180	        if response_code not in (None, 0, "0", "OK", "SUCCESS"):
0181	            if response_code in {"VERSION_CONFLICT", "CONFLICT"}:
0182	                raise RepositoryVersionConflict(_current_version_from_payload(payload))
0183	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned a dependency error")
0184	        return payload
0185	
0186	    @staticmethod
0187	    def _data(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
0188	        data = payload.get("data")
0189	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
0190	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned invalid data")
0191	        return dict(data)

0194	        self,
0195	        payload: Mapping[str, Any],
0196	        collection: str,
0197	        document_id: str,
0198	        expected_version: int,
0199	    ) -> None:
0200	        data = self._data(payload)
0201	        matched = data.get("matched")
0202	        updated = data.get("updated")
0203	        if not isinstance(matched, int) or not isinstance(updated, int):
0204	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned an invalid update result")
0205	        if matched > 0 and updated > 0:
0206	            return

0208	            current_document = await self._find_by_id(collection, document_id)
0209	        except RepositoryNotFound:
0210	            raise RepositoryNotFound("CloudBase document not found") from None
0211	        current_version = current_document.get("version")
0212	        raise RepositoryVersionConflict(
0213	            current_version if isinstance(current_version, int) else None
0214	        )

0218	    return json.dumps(
0219	        value,

0222	        separators=(",", ":"),
0223	        default=_json_default,

0228	    if isinstance(value, datetime):
0229	        return {"$date": int(value.timestamp() * 1000)}
0230	    raise TypeError(f"unsupported CloudBase value: {type(value).__name__}")

0233	def _decode_document(value: Any) -> JsonDocument:
0234	    if isinstance(value, dict):
0235	        return dict(value)
0236	    if isinstance(value, str):
0237	        try:
0238	            parsed = json.loads(value)
0239	        except ValueError as error:
0240	            raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned invalid document data") from error
0241	        if isinstance(parsed, dict):
0242	            return parsed
0243	    raise RepositoryUnavailable("CloudBase returned invalid document data")
0244

0247	    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(str(offset).encode("ascii")).decode("ascii")

0010	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0011	class FieldSpec:
0012	    name: str
0013	    storage_type: str
0014	    required: bool
0015	
0016	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
0017	        return {"storage_type": self.storage_type, "required": self.required}
0018	
0019	
0020	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0021	class IndexSpec:
0022	    fields: tuple[str, ...]
0023	    unique: bool = False
0024	
0025	    def as_dict(self, collection: str) -> dict[str, object]:
0026	        suffix = "__unique" if self.unique else ""
0027	        return {
0028	            "name": f"{collection}__{'_'.join(self.fields)}{suffix}",
0029	            "fields": list(self.fields),
0030	            "unique": self.unique,
0031	        }
0032	
0033	
0034	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0035	class CollectionSpec:
0036	    name: str
0037	    fields: tuple[FieldSpec, ...]
0038	    indexes: tuple[IndexSpec, ...]
0039	
0040	
0041	def field(name: str, storage_type: str, *, required: bool) -> FieldSpec:
0042	    return FieldSpec(name=name, storage_type=storage_type, required=required)

0487	COLLECTIONS = tuple(REGISTRY)
0488	
0489	if set(COLLECTION_MODELS) != set(REGISTRY):
0490	    missing_models = sorted(set(REGISTRY) - set(COLLECTION_MODELS))
0491	    missing_registry = sorted(set(COLLECTION_MODELS) - set(REGISTRY))
0492	    raise RuntimeError(
0493	        "collection registry and models are out of sync: "
0494	        f"missing_models={missing_models}, missing_registry={missing_registry}"
0495	    )

0497	
0498	def build_collection_projection() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
0499	    return {
0500	        name: {
0501	            "fields": {item.name: item.as_dict() for item in spec.fields},
0502	            "indexes": [index.as_dict(name) for index in spec.indexes],
0503	        }
0504	        for name, spec in REGISTRY.items()
0505	    }
0506	
0507	
0508	def build_index_projection() -> dict[str, list[dict[str, object]]]:
0509	    return {
0510	        name: [index.as_dict(name) for index in spec.indexes] for name, spec in REGISTRY.items()
0511	    }

0066	class InMemoryDomainDataRepository:
0067	    def __init__(
0068	        self,
0069	        *,
0070	        users: list[UserAccountDocument] | None = None,
0071	        consents: list[ConsentEventDocument] | None = None,
0072	        identities: list[IdentityRecordDocument] | None = None,
0073	        anonymous_identities: list[AnonymousIdentityDocument] | None = None,
0074	        assessment_modules: list[AssessmentModuleDocument] | None = None,
0075	        assessment_questionnaires: list[AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument] | None = None,
0076	        assessment_sessions: list[AssessmentSessionDocument] | None = None,
0077	        assessment_results: list[AssessmentResultDocument] | None = None,
0078	        daily_mood_records: list[DailyMoodRecordDocument] | None = None,
0079	        quote_entries: list[QuoteEntryDocument] | None = None,
0080	        support_resources: list[SupportResourceDocument] | None = None,
0081	        safety_support_tasks: list[SafetySupportTaskDocument] | None = None,
0082	        work_tasks: list[WorkTaskDocument] | None = None,
0083	        treehole_posts: list[TreeholePostDocument] | None = None,
0084	        treehole_responses: list[TreeholeResponseDocument] | None = None,
0085	        identity_access_requests: list[IdentityAccessRequestDocument] | None = None,
0086	        extra_collections: Mapping[str, list[Mapping[str, Any]]] | None = None,
0087	    ) -> None:
0088	        self._lock = RLock()
0089	        self._users = {user.document_id: user for user in users or []}
0090	        self._consents = {consent.document_id: consent for consent in consents or []}
0091	        self._identities = {identity.document_id: identity for identity in identities or []}
0092	        self._anonymous = {
0093	            anonymous_identity.document_id: anonymous_identity
0094	            for anonymous_identity in anonymous_identities or []
0095	        }
0096	        self._assessment_modules: dict[str, AssessmentModuleDocument] = {
0097	            module.module_code: module for module in assessment_modules or []
0098	        }
0099	        self._assessment_questionnaires: dict[tuple[str, str], AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument] = {
0100	            (questionnaire.module_code, questionnaire.questionnaire_version): questionnaire
0101	            for questionnaire in assessment_questionnaires or []
0102	        }
0103	        self._assessment_sessions = {
0104	            session.document_id: session for session in assessment_sessions or []
0105	        }
0106	        self._assessment_results = {
0107	            result.document_id: result for result in assessment_results or []
0108	        }
0109	        self._daily_moods = {record.document_id: record for record in daily_mood_records or []}
0110	        self._quote_entries = {quote.document_id: quote for quote in quote_entries or []}

0139	    @contextmanager
0140	    def transaction(self) -> Iterator[None]:
0141	        """Run a domain write atomically across all in-memory collections."""
0142	
0143	        with self._lock:
0144	            snapshot = self._snapshot()
0145	            try:
0146	                yield
0147	            except BaseException:
0148	                self._restore(snapshot)
0149	                raise
0150	
0151	    def _snapshot(self) -> _DomainDataSnapshot:

0155	            identities=deepcopy(self._identities),
0156	            anonymous=deepcopy(self._anonymous),
0157	            assessment_modules=deepcopy(self._assessment_modules),
0158	            assessment_questionnaires=deepcopy(self._assessment_questionnaires),
0159	            assessment_sessions=deepcopy(self._assessment_sessions),
0160	            assessment_results=deepcopy(self._assessment_results),
0161	            daily_moods=deepcopy(self._daily_moods),
0162	            quote_entries=deepcopy(self._quote_entries),
0163	            support_resources=deepcopy(self._support_resources),
0164	            safety_support_tasks=deepcopy(self._safety_support_tasks),

0227	    def create_user(self, user: UserAccountDocument) -> UserAccountDocument:
0228	        with self._lock:
0229	            if user.document_id in self._users:
0230	                raise RepositoryVersionConflict(self._users[user.document_id].version)
0231	            if self.get_user_by_auth_subject_hash(user.auth_subject_hash) is not None:
0232	                raise RepositoryVersionConflict(1)
0233	            self._users[user.document_id] = user
0234	            return user

0001	"""Atomic in-memory idempotency records matching the CloudBase collection shape."""
0002	
0003	from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
0004	from datetime import datetime
0005	from threading import RLock
0006	from typing import Literal, Protocol
0007	
0008	IdempotencyOutcome = Literal["processing", "success", "failure"]
0009	
0010	
0011	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0012	class IdempotencyRecord:
0013	    record_id: str
0014	    actor_type: str
0015	    actor_id: str
0016	    route_key: str
0017	    idempotency_key: str
0018	    request_hash: str
0019	    outcome: IdempotencyOutcome
0020	    response_status: int | None
0021	    response_digest: str | None
0022	    expires_at: datetime
0023	    created_at: datetime
0024	    updated_at: datetime
0025	    version: int = 1
0026	
0027	
0028	class IdempotencyRepository(Protocol):

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/idempotency_repository.py ===
0029	    def get(
0030	        self,
0031	        actor_type: str,
0032	        actor_id: str,
0033	        route_key: str,
0034	        idempotency_key: str,
0035	        *,
0036	        now: datetime,
0037	    ) -> IdempotencyRecord | None: ...
0038	
0039	    def reserve(self, record: IdempotencyRecord, *, now: datetime) -> IdempotencyRecord | None: ...
0040	
0041	    def complete(
0042	        self,
0043	        record_id: str,
0044	        *,
0045	        outcome: IdempotencyOutcome,
0046	        response_status: int,
0047	        response_digest: str,
0048	        now: datetime,
0049	    ) -> IdempotencyRecord: ...
0050	
0051	
0052	class InMemoryIdempotencyRepository:
0053	    def __init__(self) -> None:
0054	        self._records: dict[tuple[str, str, str, str], IdempotencyRecord] = {}
0055	        self._lock = RLock()
0056	
0057	    @staticmethod
0058	    def _key(record: IdempotencyRecord) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
0059	        return record.actor_type, record.actor_id, record.route_key, record.idempotency_key
0060	
0061	    def get(
0062	        self,
0063	        actor_type: str,
0064	        actor_id: str,
0065	        route_key: str,
0066	        idempotency_key: str,
0067	        *,
0068	        now: datetime,
0069	    ) -> IdempotencyRecord | None:
0070	        with self._lock:
0071	            record = self._records.get((actor_type, actor_id, route_key, idempotency_key))
0072	            if record is not None and record.expires_at <= now:
0073	                self._records.pop((actor_type, actor_id, route_key, idempotency_key), None)
0074	                return None
0075	            return record
0076	
0077	    def reserve(self, record: IdempotencyRecord, *, now: datetime) -> IdempotencyRecord | None:
0078	        with self._lock:
0079	            key = self._key(record)
0080	            existing = self._records.get(key)
0081	            if existing is not None and existing.expires_at > now:
0082	                return existing
0083	            self._records[key] = record
0084	            return None
0085	
0086	    def complete(

=== 全文第 246 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/idempotency_repository.py ===
0087	        self,
0088	        record_id: str,
0089	        *,
0090	        outcome: IdempotencyOutcome,
0091	        response_status: int,
0092	        response_digest: str,
0093	        now: datetime,
0094	    ) -> IdempotencyRecord:
0095	        with self._lock:
0096	            for key, record in self._records.items():
0097	                if record.record_id == record_id:
0098	                    if record.outcome != "processing":
0099	                        return record
0100	                    completed = replace(
0101	                        record,
0102	                        outcome=outcome,
0103	                        response_status=response_status,
0104	                        response_digest=response_digest,
0105	                        updated_at=now,
0106	                        version=record.version + 1,
0107	                    )
0108	                    self._records[key] = completed
0109	                    return completed
0110	        raise KeyError(record_id)

0001	"""Small in-memory repository used by unit tests and local development."""
0002	
0003	import base64
0004	import binascii
0005	from collections.abc import Mapping
0006	from copy import deepcopy
0007	from datetime import UTC, datetime
0008	from typing import Any
0009	
0010	from app.repositories.protocols import (
0011	    DocumentPage,
0012	    JsonDocument,
0013	    RepositoryNotFound,
0014	    RepositoryVersionConflict,
0015	)
0016	
0017	
0018	class InMemoryDocumentRepository:
0019	    def __init__(self, collections: Mapping[str, list[Mapping[str, Any]]] | None = None) -> None:
0020	        self._collections: dict[str, dict[str, JsonDocument]] = {}
0021	        for collection, documents in (collections or {}).items():
0022	            self._collections[collection] = {
0023	                str(document["_id"]): deepcopy(dict(document)) for document in documents
0024	            }
0025	
0026	    def query(
0027	        self,
0028	        collection: str,
0029	        where: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
0030	        *,
0031	        cursor: str | None = None,
0032	        limit: int = 20,

=== 全文第 247 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/in_memory.py ===
0033	    ) -> DocumentPage:
0034	        documents = list(self._collections.get(collection, {}).values())
0035	        filters = where or {}
0036	        matched = [
0037	            document
0038	            for document in documents
0039	            if not document.get("is_deleted", False)
0040	            and all(document.get(key) == value for key, value in filters.items())
0041	        ]
0042	        matched.sort(key=lambda document: str(document.get("_id", "")))
0043	        offset = self._decode_cursor(cursor)
0044	        page_size = max(1, min(limit, 100))
0045	        page = matched[offset : offset + page_size]
0046	        next_offset = offset + page_size
0047	        next_cursor = self._encode_cursor(next_offset) if next_offset < len(matched) else None
0048	        return DocumentPage(tuple(deepcopy(item) for item in page), next_cursor)
0049	
0050	    def conditional_update(
0051	        self,
0052	        collection: str,
0053	        document_id: str,
0054	        *,
0055	        expected_version: int,
0056	        updates: Mapping[str, Any],
0057	    ) -> JsonDocument:
0058	        document = self._get(collection, document_id)
0059	        current_version = int(document.get("version", 1))
0060	        if current_version != expected_version:
0061	            raise RepositoryVersionConflict(current_version)
0062	        if "_id" in updates or "version" in updates:
0063	            raise ValueError("_id and version are managed by the repository")
0064	        document.update(deepcopy(dict(updates)))
0065	        document["version"] = current_version + 1
0066	        document["updated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
0067	        return deepcopy(document)
0068	
0069	    def logical_delete(
0070	        self,
0071	        collection: str,
0072	        document_id: str,
0073	        *,
0074	        expected_version: int,
0075	    ) -> JsonDocument:
0076	        document = self._get(collection, document_id)
0077	        current_version = int(document.get("version", 1))
0078	        if current_version != expected_version:
0079	            raise RepositoryVersionConflict(current_version)
0080	        if not document.get("is_deleted", False):
0081	            document["is_deleted"] = True
0082	            document["deleted_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
0083	            document["version"] = current_version + 1
0084	            document["updated_at"] = document["deleted_at"]

=== 全文第 248 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/in_memory.py ===
0085	        return deepcopy(document)
0086	
0087	    def _get(self, collection: str, document_id: str) -> JsonDocument:
0088	        document = self._collections.get(collection, {}).get(document_id)
0089	        if document is None:
0090	            raise RepositoryNotFound("document not found")
0091	        return document
0092	
0093	    @staticmethod
0094	    def _encode_cursor(offset: int) -> str:
0095	        return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(str(offset).encode("ascii")).decode("ascii")
0096	
0097	    @staticmethod
0098	    def _decode_cursor(cursor: str | None) -> int:
0099	        if not cursor:
0100	            return 0
0101	        try:
0102	            value = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(cursor.encode("ascii")).decode("ascii")
0103	            offset = int(value)
0104	        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError, binascii.Error) as error:
0105	            raise ValueError("invalid cursor") from error
0106	        if offset < 0:
0107	            raise ValueError("invalid cursor")
0108	        return offset

0011	class DocumentPage:
0012	    items: tuple[JsonDocument, ...]
0013	    next_cursor: str | None
0014	
0015	
0016	class RepositoryError(RuntimeError):
0017	    """Base class for errors that must be mapped at the API boundary."""
0018	
0019	
0020	class RepositoryNotFound(RepositoryError):
0021	    """The requested document does not exist in the current environment."""
0022	
0023	
0024	class RepositoryVersionConflict(RepositoryError):
0025	    """The document version changed before a conditional update."""
0026	
0027	    def __init__(self, current_version: int | None) -> None:
0028	        self.current_version = current_version
0029	        super().__init__("document version conflict")
0030	
0031	
0032	class RepositoryUnavailable(RepositoryError):
0033	    """CloudBase or another persistence dependency cannot be reached."""
0034	
0035

0044	    ) -> DocumentPage: ...
0045

0047	        self,
0048	        collection: str,
0049	        document_id: str,
0050	        *,

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/session_repository.py ===
0001	"""Server-side session records; raw access and refresh tokens are never stored."""
0002	
0003	from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
0004	from datetime import datetime
0005	from threading import RLock
0006	from typing import Literal
0007	
0008	SubjectType = Literal["student", "admin"]
0009	SessionStatus = Literal["active", "revoked"]
0010	
0011	
0012	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0013	class AuthSessionRecord:
0014	    session_id: str
0015	    subject_type: SubjectType
0016	    subject_id: str
0017	    capability: str | None
0018	    access_token_hash: str
0019	    refresh_token_hash: str
0020	    access_expires_at: datetime
0021	    refresh_expires_at: datetime
0022	    status: SessionStatus
0023	    created_at: datetime
0024	    updated_at: datetime
0025	    version: int = 1
0026	
0027	
0028	class InMemorySessionRepository:
0029	    def __init__(self) -> None:
0030	        self._records: dict[str, AuthSessionRecord] = {}
0031	        self._access_index: dict[str, str] = {}
0032	        self._refresh_index: dict[str, str] = {}
0033	        self._lock = RLock()
0034	
0035	    def save(self, record: AuthSessionRecord) -> None:
0036	        with self._lock:
0037	            self._records[record.session_id] = record
0038	            self._access_index[record.access_token_hash] = record.session_id
0039	            self._refresh_index[record.refresh_token_hash] = record.session_id
0040	
0041	    def replace(self, record: AuthSessionRecord) -> None:
0042	        with self._lock:
0043	            old = self._records.get(record.session_id)
0044	            if old is not None:
0045	                self._access_index.pop(old.access_token_hash, None)
0046	                self._refresh_index.pop(old.refresh_token_hash, None)
0047	            self.save(record)
0048	
0049	    def get_by_session_id(self, session_id: str) -> AuthSessionRecord | None:
0050	        with self._lock:

=== 全文第 250 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/repositories/session_repository.py ===
0051	            return self._records.get(session_id)
0052	
0053	    def get_by_access_token_hash(self, token_hash: str) -> AuthSessionRecord | None:
0054	        with self._lock:
0055	            session_id = self._access_index.get(token_hash)
0056	            return self._records.get(session_id) if session_id else None
0057	
0058	    def get_by_refresh_token_hash(self, token_hash: str) -> AuthSessionRecord | None:
0059	        with self._lock:
0060	            session_id = self._refresh_index.get(token_hash)
0061	            return self._records.get(session_id) if session_id else None
0062	
0063	    def revoke(self, session_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> AuthSessionRecord | None:
0064	        with self._lock:
0065	            record = self._records.get(session_id)
0066	            if record is None:
0067	                return None
0068	            if record.status == "revoked":
0069	                return record
0070	            revoked = replace(record, status="revoked", updated_at=now, version=record.version + 1)
0071	            self._records[session_id] = revoked
0072	            return revoked

0001	"""The single response envelope shared by every HTTP endpoint."""
0002	
0003	from typing import Generic, TypeVar
0004	
0005	from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict
0006	
0007	from app.schemas.errors import ApiError
0008	
0009	DataT = TypeVar("DataT")
0010	
0011	
0012	class ApiEnvelope(BaseModel, Generic[DataT]):
0013	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
0014	
0015	    request_id: str
0016	    data: DataT | None = None
0017	    error: ApiError | None = None
0018	
0019	    @classmethod
0020	    def success(cls, request_id: str, data: DataT) -> "ApiEnvelope[DataT]":
0021	        return cls(request_id=request_id, data=data, error=None)
0022	
0023	    @classmethod
0024	    def failure(cls, request_id: str, error: ApiError) -> "ApiEnvelope[DataT]":
0025	        return cls(request_id=request_id, data=None, error=error)

=== 文件：backend/app/schemas/errors.py ===
0022	    "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE": "相关服务暂时不可用，请稍后再试",

0025	RETRYABLE_CODES = {
0026	    "INTERNAL_ERROR",
0027	    "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
0028	    "RATE_LIMITED",
0029	}
0030	
0031	
0032	class ApiError(BaseModel):
0033	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
0034	
0035	    code: str
0036	    message: str
0037	    retryable: bool = False
0038	    current_version: int | str | None = None
0039	
0040	
0041	class ApiException(Exception):
0042	    """An expected failure that can safely cross the HTTP boundary."""
0043	
0044	    def __init__(
0045	        self,
0046	        status_code: int,
0047	        code: str,
0048	        message: str | None = None,
0049	        *,
0050	        retryable: bool | None = None,
0051	        current_version: int | str | None = None,
0052	    ) -> None:
0053	        self.status_code = status_code
0054	        self.code = code
0055	        self.message = message or ERROR_MESSAGES.get(code, ERROR_MESSAGES["INTERNAL_ERROR"])
0056	        self.retryable = code in RETRYABLE_CODES if retryable is None else retryable
0057	        self.current_version = current_version
0058	        super().__init__(self.message)
0059	
0060	    def to_error(self) -> ApiError:
0061	        return ApiError(
0062	            code=self.code,
0063	            message=self.message,
0064	            retryable=self.retryable,
0065	            current_version=self.current_version,
0066	        )
0067	
0068	
0069	def status_error(status_code: int) -> ApiException:
0070	    mapping = {
0071	        400: "INVALID_REQUEST",
0072	        401: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
0073	        403: "FORBIDDEN",
0074	        404: "NOT_FOUND",
0075	        409: "VERSION_CONFLICT",
0076	        422: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
0077	        429: "RATE_LIMITED",
0078	        500: "INTERNAL_ERROR",
0079	        503: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
0080	    }
0081	    return ApiException(status_code, mapping.get(status_code, "INTERNAL_ERROR"))
0082	
0083	
0084	def safe_validation_error() -> ApiException:

0085	    return ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")

0088	def safe_error_payload(error: ApiException) -> dict[str, Any]:
0089	    return error.to_error().model_dump()

=== 文件：backend/app/security/passwords.py ===
0001	"""Password hashing helpers for the single fixed admin account."""
0002	
0003	import base64
0004	import hashlib
0005	import hmac
0006	import secrets
0007	
0008	PASSWORD_SCHEME = "pbkdf2_sha256"
0009	PASSWORD_ITERATIONS = 310_000
0010	
0011	
0012	def hash_password(password: str, *, salt: bytes | None = None) -> str:
0013	    if not password:
0014	        raise ValueError("password cannot be empty")
0015	    actual_salt = salt or secrets.token_bytes(16)
0016	    digest = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
0017	        "sha256",
0018	        password.encode("utf-8"),
0019	        actual_salt,
0020	        PASSWORD_ITERATIONS,
0021	    )
0022	
0023	    def encode(value: bytes) -> str:
0024	        return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(value).decode("ascii").rstrip("=")
0025	
0026	    return f"{PASSWORD_SCHEME}${PASSWORD_ITERATIONS}${encode(actual_salt)}${encode(digest)}"
0027	
0028	
0029	def verify_password(password: str, encoded_hash: str) -> bool:
0030	    try:
0031	        scheme, iteration_text, salt_text, digest_text = encoded_hash.split("$", 3)
0032	        if scheme != PASSWORD_SCHEME:
0033	            return False
0034	        iterations = int(iteration_text)
0035	        salt = _decode(salt_text)
0036	        expected = _decode(digest_text)
0037	    except (AttributeError, ValueError):
0038	        return False
0039	    if iterations < 100_000 or not salt or not expected:
0040	        return False
0041	    actual = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode("utf-8"), salt, iterations)
0042	    return hmac.compare_digest(actual, expected)
0043	
0044	
0045	def _decode(value: str) -> bytes:
0046	    padding = "=" * (-len(value) % 4)
0047	    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(value + padding)

0043	
0044	    def __init__(self, secret: str) -> None:
0045	        if not secret:
0046	            raise ValueError("token secret cannot be empty")
0047	        self._secret = secret.encode("utf-8")
0048	
0049	    def issue(
0050	        self,
0051	        subject_type: TokenSubjectType,
0052	        subject_id: str,
0053	        repository: InMemorySessionRepository,
0054	        *,
0055	        capability: str | None = None,
0056	        now: datetime | None = None,
0057	    ) -> TokenPair:
0058	        issued_at = _utc(now or datetime.now(UTC))
0059	        access_ttl, refresh_ttl = self._ttls(subject_type)
0060	        access_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
0061	        refresh_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
0062	        pair = TokenPair(
0063	            session_id=f"sess_{uuid4().hex}",
0064	            access_token=access_token,
0065	            refresh_token=refresh_token,
0066	            access_expires_at=issued_at + access_ttl,
0067	            refresh_expires_at=issued_at + refresh_ttl,
0068	        )
0069	        repository.save(
0070	            AuthSessionRecord(
0071	                session_id=pair.session_id,

0072	                subject_type=subject_type,
0073	                subject_id=subject_id,
0074	                capability=capability,

0076	                refresh_token_hash=self.hash_token(refresh_token),
0077	                access_expires_at=pair.access_expires_at,

0086	    def authenticate_access(
0087	        self,
0088	        access_token: str,
0089	        repository: InMemorySessionRepository,
0090	        *,
0091	        now: datetime | None = None,
0092	    ) -> AuthenticatedSubject:
0093	        record = repository.get_by_access_token_hash(self.hash_token(access_token))
0094	        current = _utc(now or datetime.now(UTC))
0095	        if record is None:
0096	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0097	        if record.status != "active" or record.access_expires_at <= current:
0098	            raise ApiException(401, "SESSION_EXPIRED")
0099	        return AuthenticatedSubject(
0100	            session_id=record.session_id,
0101	            subject_type=record.subject_type,
0102	            subject_id=record.subject_id,
0103	            capability=record.capability,
0104	            session_expires_at=record.access_expires_at,
0105	        )
0106	
0107	    def refresh(
0108	        self,
0109	        refresh_token: str,
0110	        repository: InMemorySessionRepository,
0111	        *,
0112	        expected_subject_type: TokenSubjectType | None = None,
0113	        now: datetime | None = None,
0114	    ) -> TokenPair:
0115	        current = _utc(now or datetime.now(UTC))
0116	        record = repository.get_by_refresh_token_hash(self.hash_token(refresh_token))
0117	        if record is None or record.status != "active" or record.refresh_expires_at <= current:
0118	            raise ApiException(401, "SESSION_EXPIRED")
0119	        if expected_subject_type is not None and record.subject_type != expected_subject_type:
0120	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0121	
0122	        access_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
0123	        new_refresh_token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
0124	        access_ttl, refresh_ttl = self._ttls(record.subject_type)

0125	        pair = TokenPair(
0126	            session_id=record.session_id,
0127	            access_token=access_token,
0128	            refresh_token=new_refresh_token,

0130	            refresh_expires_at=current + refresh_ttl,
0131	        )

0141	                refresh_expires_at=pair.refresh_expires_at,
0142	                status="active",
0143	                created_at=record.created_at,
0144	                updated_at=current,
0145	                version=record.version + 1,
0146	            )

0150	    def logout(
0151	        self,
0152	        access_token: str,
0153	        repository: InMemorySessionRepository,
0154	        *,
0155	        expected_subject_type: TokenSubjectType | None = None,
0156	        now: datetime | None = None,
0157	    ) -> None:
0158	        record = repository.get_by_access_token_hash(self.hash_token(access_token))
0159	        if record is None:
0160	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0161	        if expected_subject_type is not None and record.subject_type != expected_subject_type:
0162	            raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0163	        repository.revoke(record.session_id, now=_utc(now or datetime.now(UTC)))
0164	
0165	    def hash_token(self, token: str) -> str:
0166	        return hmac.new(self._secret, token.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
0167	
0168	    @classmethod
0169	    def _ttls(cls, subject_type: TokenSubjectType) -> tuple[timedelta, timedelta]:
0170	        if subject_type == "student":
0171	            return cls.STUDENT_ACCESS_TTL, cls.STUDENT_REFRESH_TTL
0172	        return cls.ADMIN_ACCESS_TTL, cls.ADMIN_REFRESH_TTL
0173	
0174	
0175	def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
0176	    if value.tzinfo is None:
0177	        return value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
0178	    return value.astimezone(UTC)

0069	    def _transition(
0070	        self,
0071	        access_token: str,
0072	        *,
0073	        target: str,
0074	        object_version: int,
0075	        request_id: str,
0076	        idempotency_key: str,
0077	    ) -> AccountState:
0078	        subject = self.tokens.authenticate_access(access_token, self.sessions)
0079	        if subject.subject_type != "student":
0080	            raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0081	        reservation = self.idempotency.begin(
0082	            "student",
0083	            subject.subject_id,
0084	            f"/account/{target}",
0085	            idempotency_key,
0086	            {"object_version": object_version},
0087	        )
0088	        if reservation.replayed:
0089	            return _state_from_digest(reservation.record.response_digest)
0090	        try:
0091	            with self.repository.transaction():
0092	                user = self.repository.get_user(subject.subject_id)
0093	                if user.version != object_version:
0094	                    raise ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=user.version)
0095	                now = datetime.now(UTC)
0096	                if target == "recovery_pending":
0097	                    if user.status != "active":
0098	                        raise ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=user.version)
0099	                    updated = user.model_copy(
0100	                        update={
0101	                            "status": "recovery_pending",
0102	                            "stop_requested_at": now,
0103	                            "recovery_deadline_at": now + timedelta(days=30),
0104	                        }
0105	                    )
0106	                else:
0107	                    if user.status != "recovery_pending":
0108	                        raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0109	                    if user.recovery_deadline_at is not None and now > user.recovery_deadline_at:

=== 全文第 266 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/services/account_service.py ===
0110	                        raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0111	                    updated = user.model_copy(
0112	                        update={
0113	                            "status": "active",
0114	                            "stop_requested_at": None,
0115	                            "recovery_deadline_at": None,
0116	                        }
0117	                    )
0118	                saved = self.repository.save_user(updated, expected_version=user.version)

0137	        except Exception as error:

0139	            _complete_failure(self.idempotency, reservation, failure)

0143	                request_id=request_id,
0144	                actor_type="student",
0145	                actor_id=subject.subject_id,
0146	                capability=None,
0147	                action=f"account_{target}",
0148	                resource_type="user",
0149	                resource_id=subject.subject_id,
0150	                data_scope="necessary_facts",
0151	                outcome="success",
0152	                reason_code=target,
0153	                occurred_at=datetime.now(UTC),
0154	                facts={"action_code": target, "object_version": state.object_version},
0155	            )

0161	    def _state(user: UserAccountDocument) -> AccountState:

0174	def _state_from_digest(value: str | None) -> AccountState:
0175	    if value is None:
0176	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR")
0177	    error = deserialize_api_error(value)
0178	    if error is not None:
0179	        raise error
0180	    try:
0181	        return AccountState.model_validate_json(value)
0182	    except Exception as exc:
0183	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR") from exc

0190	        reservation,
0191	        status_code=error.status_code,
0192	        response_digest=serialize_api_error(error),
0193	        outcome="failure",
0194	    )

0297	    def _snapshot(
0298	        self,
0299	        *,
0300	        task_type: TaskType,
0301	        resource_type: Literal["assessment_result", "treehole_post", "treehole_response"],
0302	        resource_id: str,
0303	        owner_user_id: str | None,
0304	        input_data: Mapping[str, Any],
0305	        output_status: Literal["adopted", "fallback", "rejected"],
0306	        output_projection: dict[str, Any] | None,
0307	        adopted_copy: str | None,
0308	    ) -> AiAssistSnapshot:
0309	        now = datetime.now(UTC)
0310	        snapshot = AiAssistSnapshot(
0311	            snapshot_id=f"ai_{uuid4().hex}",
0312	            task_type=task_type,
0313	            resource_type=resource_type,
0314	            resource_id=resource_id,
0315	            owner_user_id=owner_user_id,
0316	            input_digest=hashlib.sha256(
0317	                json.dumps(dict(input_data), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True).encode()
0318	            ).hexdigest(),
0319	            output_status=output_status,
0320	            output_projection=output_projection,
0321	            adopted_copy=adopted_copy,
0322	            created_at=now,
0323	            updated_at=now,
0324	        )
0325	        self.snapshots[snapshot.snapshot_id] = snapshot
0326	        return snapshot
0327

0329	        self.audit.write(

0331	            actor_type="system",

0335	            resource_type="ai_assist",
0336	            resource_id=task_type,
0337	            data_scope="task_type,model_version,prompt_version,outcome_category,error_category",
0338	            outcome="success" if outcome == "adopted" else "failure",
0339	            reason_code=reason,
0340	            occurred_at=datetime.now(UTC),
0341	            facts={
0342	                "task_kind": task_type,
0343	                "model_version": RESOLVED_MODEL_VERSION,
0344	                "prompt_version": PROMPT_VERSION,
0345	                "outcome_category": outcome,
0346	                "error_category": reason,

0347	            },

0767	        self,
0768	        module_code: str,
0769	    ) -> tuple[
0770	        AssessmentModuleDocument,
0771	        AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument,
0772	        tuple[CatalogQuestion, ...],
0773	    ]:
0774	        module = self.repository.get_assessment_module(module_code)

0784	    @staticmethod
0785	    def _validate_questionnaire(
0786	        module: AssessmentModuleDocument,
0787	        questionnaire: AssessmentQuestionnaireDocument,
0788	    ) -> tuple[CatalogQuestion, ...]:
0789	        if (
0790	            not questionnaire.enabled
0791	            or questionnaire.module_code != module.module_code
0792	            or questionnaire.questionnaire_version != module.current_questionnaire_version
0793	        ):
0794	            raise ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0795	        try:
0796	            return questionnaire_questions(questionnaire)

0800	    def _ensure_assessment_access(self, user_id: str) -> None:
0801	        user = self.repository.get_user(user_id)
0802	        if user.status != "active":
0803	            raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0804	        if user.base_consent_status != "accepted":
0805	            raise ApiException(403, "CONSENT_REQUIRED")
0806	        if not user.identity_record_id:
0807	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")
0808	        try:
0809	            identity = self.repository.get_identity_record(user.identity_record_id)
0810	        except RepositoryNotFound as error:
0811	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED") from error
0812	        if identity.user_id != user.document_id or identity.verification_status != "verified":
0813	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")

0905	def _result_projection(result: AssessmentResultDocument) -> AssessmentResultProjection:
0906	    # The document validator rejects ``cannot_be_safe`` results. Keep the
0907	    # invariant explicit here so the public projection never exposes it.
0908	    if result.safety_state == "cannot_be_safe":
0909	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR")
0910	    is_safety_support = result.result_state == "safety_support"
0911	    return AssessmentResultProjection(
0912	        result_id=result.document_id,
0913	        session_id=result.session_id,
0914	        module_code=result.module_code,
0915	        result_state=result.result_state,
0916	        score=None if is_safety_support else result.score,
0917	        fixed_summary=result.fixed_summary,
0918	        reference_band=None if is_safety_support else result.reference_band,
0919	        boundary_notice=result.boundary_notice,
0920	        dimension_summary=result.dimension_summary,
0921	        safety_state=result.safety_state,
0922	        visible_copy_version=result.visible_copy_version,
0923	        created_at=result.created_at,
0924	        updated_at=result.updated_at,
0925	        object_version=result.version,
0926	    )

0929	def _decode_assessment_cursor(cursor: str | None) -> int:

=== 全文第 292 页 / 365 页 ===

0931	        return 0
0932	    try:
0933	        value = int(cursor)
0934	    except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
0935	        raise ValueError("invalid cursor") from error
0936	    if value < 0:
0937	        raise ValueError("invalid cursor")
0938	    return value

0946	        raise error
0947	    try:
0948	        return AssessmentResultDeleteResponse.model_validate_json(value)
0949	    except Exception as exc:
0950	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR") from exc

0048	    async def login_student(self, code: str, *, client_version: str) -> StudentSessionData:
0049	        del client_version
0050	        self._require_ready()
0051	        identity = await self.wechat.exchange_code(code)

0053	        pair = self.tokens.issue("student", identity.subject_id, self.sessions)

0062	            ),

0064	        )

0066	    def login_admin(self, login_name: str, password: str) -> AdminSessionData:
0067	        self._require_ready()
0068	        if login_name != self.ADMIN_LOGIN_NAME or not self.settings.password_hash:
0069	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0070	        if not verify_password(password, self.settings.password_hash):
0071	            raise ApiException(401, "AUTH_REQUIRED")
0072	        pair = self.tokens.issue(
0073	            "admin",
0074	            "admin:fixed-super-admin",
0075	            self.sessions,
0076	            capability=self.ADMIN_CAPABILITY,
0077	        )
0078	        return AdminSessionData(
0079	            **_pair_data(pair),
0080	            display_name=self.ADMIN_DISPLAY_NAME,
0081	            capability_label=self.ADMIN_CAPABILITY_LABEL,
0082	        )

0042	        current = _utc(now or datetime.now(UTC))

0094	
0095	def serialize_api_error(error: ApiException) -> str:
0096	    """Serialize only the safe API error contract for a failure replay."""
0097	
0098	    return json.dumps(
0099	        {
0100	            "status_code": error.status_code,
0101	            "error": error.to_error().model_dump(),
0102	        },
0103	        ensure_ascii=False,
0104	        sort_keys=True,
0105	        separators=(",", ":"),
0106	    )
0107	
0108	
0109	def deserialize_api_error(response_digest: str | None) -> ApiException | None:
0110	    """Restore a safe API error digest, while tolerating legacy failure digests."""
0111	
0112	    if response_digest is None:
0113	        return None
0114	    try:
0115	        data = json.loads(response_digest)
0116	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
0117	            return None
0118	        if "error" in data:
0119	            status_code = data.get("status_code")
0120	            error_data = data["error"]
0121	            if type(status_code) is not int or not isinstance(error_data, dict):
0122	                return None
0123	            error = ApiError.model_validate(error_data)
0124	            return ApiException(
0125	                status_code,
0126	                error.code,
0127	                error.message,
0128	                retryable=error.retryable,
0129	                current_version=error.current_version,
0130	            )
0131	        if "error_code" in data:
0132	            return ApiException(int(data["status_code"]), str(data["error_code"]))
0133	    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
0134	        return None
0135	    return None
0136	
0137	
0138	def _hash_request(request_body: Any) -> str:
0139	    canonical = json.dumps(
0140	        request_body,
0141	        ensure_ascii=False,
0142	        sort_keys=True,
0143	        separators=(",", ":"),
0144	        default=str,
0145	    )
0146	    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
0147	
0148	
0149	def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
0150	    if value.tzinfo is None:
0151	        return value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
0152	    return value.astimezone(UTC)

0042	IdentityVerificationStatus = Literal["not_started", "pending", "verified", "failed", "unavailable"]
0043	StudentSessionIdentityStatus = Literal["unverified", "pending", "verified"]

0045	
0046	class IdentityCipher(Protocol):
0047	    def encrypt(self, value: str) -> str: ...
0048	
0049	
0050	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
0051	class IdentityVerificationState:
0052	    verification_status: IdentityVerificationStatus
0053	    identity_record_id: str
0054	    anonymous_identity_id: str | None
0055	
0056	
0057	class HmacIdentityCipher:
0058	    """Small local authenticated-encryption boundary for identity fields.
0059	
0060	    The application keeps the key outside the document store. A random nonce,
0061	    HMAC-derived keystream, and authentication tag keep plaintext out of the
0062	    stored value while avoiding a dependency on a crypto package in this phase.
0063	    Production deployments should replace this boundary with managed AEAD.
0064	    """
0065	
0066	    NONCE_SIZE = 16
0067	    TAG_SIZE = hashlib.sha256().digest_size
0068	
0069	    def __init__(self, secret: str) -> None:
0070	        if not secret:
0071	            raise ValueError("identity cipher secret cannot be empty")
0072	        secret_bytes = secret.encode("utf-8")
0073	        self._encryption_key = hmac.new(
0074	            secret_bytes,
0075	            b"xinyu/identity/encryption/v1",
0076	            hashlib.sha256,
0077	        ).digest()
0078	        self._authentication_key = hmac.new(
0079	            secret_bytes,
0080	            b"xinyu/identity/authentication/v1",
0081	            hashlib.sha256,
0082	        ).digest()
0083	
0084	    def encrypt(self, value: str) -> str:
0085	        nonce = secrets.token_bytes(self.NONCE_SIZE)
0086	        plaintext = value.encode("utf-8")
0087	        keystream = _hmac_keystream(self._encryption_key, nonce, len(plaintext))
0088	        ciphertext = bytes(left ^ right for left, right in zip(plaintext, keystream))
0089	        tag = hmac.new(
0090	            self._authentication_key,
0091	            nonce + ciphertext,
0092	            hashlib.sha256,
0093	        ).digest()
0094	        encoded = _urlsafe_encode(nonce + ciphertext + tag)
0095	        return f"enc:v1:{encoded}"
0096	
0097	    def decrypt(self, value: str) -> str:
0098	        if not value.startswith("enc:v1:"):
0099	            raise ValueError("unsupported identity ciphertext")
0100	        try:
0101	            raw = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(value[7:].encode("ascii"))
0102	        except (ValueError, UnicodeEncodeError) as error:
0103	            raise ValueError("invalid identity ciphertext") from error

=== 全文第 313 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/services/identity_service.py ===
0104	        if len(raw) < self.NONCE_SIZE + self.TAG_SIZE:
0105	            raise ValueError("invalid identity ciphertext")
0106	        nonce = raw[: self.NONCE_SIZE]
0107	        ciphertext = raw[self.NONCE_SIZE : -self.TAG_SIZE]
0108	        tag = raw[-self.TAG_SIZE :]
0109	        expected = hmac.new(
0110	            self._authentication_key,
0111	            nonce + ciphertext,
0112	            hashlib.sha256,
0113	        ).digest()
0114	        if not hmac.compare_digest(tag, expected):
0115	            raise ValueError("identity ciphertext authentication failed")
0116	        keystream = _hmac_keystream(self._encryption_key, nonce, len(ciphertext))
0117	        return bytes(left ^ right for left, right in zip(ciphertext, keystream)).decode("utf-8")

0470	SAFE_FAILED_REASON_CODES = frozenset({"mismatch", "invalid_request", "provider_rejected"})

0473	def _safe_failed_reason_code(verification: SchoolIdentityVerificationResult) -> str | None:
0474	    if verification.status != "failed" or not verification.failed_reason_code:
0475	        return None
0476	    if verification.failed_reason_code in SAFE_FAILED_REASON_CODES:
0477	        return verification.failed_reason_code
0478	    return "provider_rejected"

0481	def _identity_request_fingerprint(
0482	    secret: str,
0483	    *,

0485	    student_number: str,
0486	    user_version: int,
0487	) -> dict[str, object]:
0488	    return {
0489	        "student_name_digest": _keyed_digest(secret, "student_name", student_name),
0490	        "student_number_digest": _keyed_digest(secret, "student_number", student_number),
0491	        "user_version": user_version,
0492	    }

0495	def _keyed_digest(secret: str, field_name: str, value: str) -> str:
0496	    message = f"{field_name}\x00{value}".encode()
0497	    return hmac.new(secret.encode("utf-8"), message, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()

0514	    repository: InMemoryDomainDataRepository,
0515	    user: UserAccountDocument,
0516	) -> IdentityRecordDocument | None:
0517	    if not user.identity_record_id:

0521	    except RepositoryNotFound:
0522	        return None
0523	    if record.user_id != user.document_id:

0548	def _repository_failure(error: RepositoryError) -> ApiException:
0549	    if isinstance(error, RepositoryNotFound):
0550	        return ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0551	    return ApiException(503, "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE")

0554	def _hmac_keystream(key: bytes, nonce: bytes, length: int) -> bytes:
0555	    blocks = [
0556	        hmac.new(key, nonce + counter.to_bytes(4, "big"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
0557	        for counter in range((length + 31) // 32)
0558	    ]
0559	    return b"".join(blocks)[:length]
0560	
0561	
0562	def _urlsafe_encode(value: bytes) -> str:
0563	    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(value).decode("ascii")
0564	
0565	
0566	def _generate_display_name(user_id: str) -> str:
0567	    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"anonymous:{user_id}".encode()).hexdigest()[:6].upper()
0568	    return f"树洞同学{digest}"
0569	
0570	
0571	def _verification_state_from_digest(response_digest: str | None) -> IdentityVerificationState:
0572	    if response_digest is None:
0573	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR")
0574	    try:
0575	        error = deserialize_api_error(response_digest)
0576	        if error is not None:
0577	            raise error
0578	        data = json.loads(response_digest)
0579	        return IdentityVerificationState(**data)
0580	    except ApiException:
0581	        raise
0582	    except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as error:
0583	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR") from error

0402	def _encode_cursor(offset: int) -> str:
0403	    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(json.dumps(offset).encode("ascii")).decode("ascii")

0408	        return 0

0412	        raise ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED") from error
0413	    if offset < 0:
0414	        raise ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")

0059	            status="available",

0115	    def _assessment_shortcuts(self, user_id: str) -> list[AssessmentShortcut]:
0116	        ordinary_results: dict[
0117	            Literal["phq9", "gad7", "sleep_observation"],
0118	            AssessmentResultDocument,
0119	        ] = {}
0120	        safety_entry_required = False
0121	        for result in self.repository.list_assessment_results_by_user(user_id):
0122	            if result.result_state == "safety_support":
0123	                safety_entry_required = True
0124	                continue
0125	            if result.module_code not in ordinary_results:
0126	                ordinary_results[result.module_code] = result
0127	        shortcuts: list[AssessmentShortcut] = []
0128	        for module_code, (title, description) in ASSESSMENT_SHORTCUTS.items():
0129	            selected_result = ordinary_results.get(
0130	                cast(Literal["phq9", "gad7", "sleep_observation"], module_code)
0131	            )
0132	            completed_date = (
0133	                _shanghai_date(selected_result.created_at) if selected_result is not None else None
0134	            )
0135	            shortcuts.append(
0136	                AssessmentShortcut(
0137	                    module_code=module_code,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
0138	                    title=title,
0139	                    description=description,
0140	                    latest_completed_date=completed_date,
0141	                    latest_completed_text=completed_date or "尚未记录",
0142	                    safety_entry_required=safety_entry_required and module_code == "phq9",
0143	                )
0144	            )
0145	        return shortcuts

0147	    def _ensure_private_access(self, user_id: str) -> None:
0148	        user = self.repository.get_user(user_id)
0149	        if user.status != "active":
0150	            raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0151	        if user.base_consent_status != "accepted":
0152	            raise ApiException(403, "CONSENT_REQUIRED")
0153	        if not user.identity_record_id:
0154	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")
0155	        try:
0156	            identity = self.repository.get_identity_record(user.identity_record_id)
0157	        except RepositoryNotFound as error:
0158	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED") from error
0159	        if identity.user_id != user.document_id or identity.verification_status != "verified":
0160	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")

0163	        if callable(self._now_provider):

=== 全文第 335 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/services/today_service.py ===
0164	            value = self._now_provider()
0165	            if isinstance(value, datetime):
0166	                return value
0167	        return datetime.now(UTC)

0169	    def _today(self) -> str:
0170	        return _shanghai_date(self._now())

0173	def _shanghai_date(value: datetime) -> str:
0174	    if value.tzinfo is None:
0175	        value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
0176	    return value.astimezone(SHANGHAI).date().isoformat()

0363	        if option_scores[PHQ9_Q9_KEY][submitted[-1][1]] == 0:

0438	    def _ensure_assessment_access(self, user_id: str) -> None:
0439	        user = self.repository.get_user(user_id)
0440	        if user.status != "active":
0441	            raise ApiException(403, "FORBIDDEN")
0442	        if user.base_consent_status != "accepted":
0443	            raise ApiException(403, "CONSENT_REQUIRED")
0444	        if not user.identity_record_id:
0445	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")
0446	        try:
0447	            identity = self.repository.get_identity_record(user.identity_record_id)
0448	        except RepositoryNotFound as error:
0449	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED") from error
0450	        if identity.user_id != user.document_id or identity.verification_status != "verified":
0451	            raise ApiException(403, "IDENTITY_REQUIRED")

0483	    ) -> SafetyConfirmationResponse:

0488	            raise error

0492	        if response_digest is None:
0493	            raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR")

0500	def _complete_failure(
0501	    idempotency: IdempotencyService,
0502	    reservation: IdempotencyReservation,
0503	    error: ApiException,
0504	) -> None:
0505	    idempotency.complete(
0506	        reservation,
0507	        status_code=error.status_code,
0508	        response_digest=serialize_api_error(error),
0509	        outcome="failure",
0510	    )

0512	
0513	def _repository_failure(error: RepositoryError) -> ApiException:
0514	    if isinstance(error, RepositoryNotFound):
0515	        return ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0516	    return ApiException(503, "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE")

0458	                self._check_owner(post.author_user_id, subject.subject_id)
0459	                if post.version != object_version:
0460	                    raise ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=post.version)
0461	                if post.deleted_at is not None:
0462	                    raise ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0463	                saved = self.repository.save_treehole_post(

=== 全文第 354 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/services/treehole_service.py ===
0464	                    post.model_copy(update={"visibility_state": target}),
0465	                    expected_version=post.version,
0466	                )
0467	                result = self._mutation(saved, subject.subject_id)

0469	                reservation, status_code=200, response_digest=result.model_dump_json()
0470	            )
0471	            self._audit(request_id, subject.subject_id, "treehole_post_withdraw", post_id, target)

0473	        except RepositoryNotFound as error:
0474	            failure = ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0475	            _complete_failure(self.idempotency, reservation, failure)

0477	        except RepositoryVersionConflict as error:
0478	            failure = ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=error.current_version)
0479	            _complete_failure(self.idempotency, reservation, failure)
0480	            raise failure from error
0481	        except ApiException as error:
0482	            _complete_failure(self.idempotency, reservation, error)
0483	            raise
0484	        except Exception as error:
0485	            failure = ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR")
0486	            _complete_failure(self.idempotency, reservation, failure)

0495	    def _post_projection(
0496	        self, post: TreeholePostDocument, *, viewer_id: str, public: bool
0497	    ) -> TreeholePostProjection:
0498	        mine = post.author_user_id == viewer_id
0499	        expose_body = post.visibility_state in {"published", "protected"}
0500	        if public and not expose_body:
0501	            expose_body = False
0502	        responses = [
0503	            _response_projection(response, viewer_id=viewer_id, public=public)
0504	            for response in self.repository.list_treehole_responses(post.document_id)
0505	            if response.deleted_at is None and (not public or response.state == "published")
0506	        ]
0507	        return TreeholePostProjection(
0508	            post_id=post.document_id,
0509	            display_name_snapshot=post.display_name_snapshot if (public or mine) else None,
0510	            body_sanitized=post.body_sanitized if expose_body else None,
0511	            visibility_state=post.visibility_state,
0512	            review_state=post.review_state,
0513	            safety_state=post.safety_state if mine else None,
0514	            created_at=post.created_at,
0515	            updated_at=post.updated_at,

=== 全文第 355 页 / 365 页 ===

=== 文件：backend/app/services/treehole_service.py ===
0516	            response_count=len(responses),
0517	            object_version=post.version,
0518	            mine=mine,
0519	            responses=responses,
0520	        )

0522	    def _mutation(self, post: TreeholePostDocument, viewer_id: str) -> TreeholeMutationResponse:
0523	        projection = self._post_projection(post, viewer_id=viewer_id, public=False)
0524	        return TreeholeMutationResponse(
0525	            post_id=post.document_id,

0527	            review_state=post.review_state,
0528	            display_projection=projection,
0529	            object_version=post.version,
0530	        )

0558	def _protected_body(body: str) -> str:
0559	    return "enc:v1:" + hashlib.sha256(body.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

0562	def _contains_safety_signal(body: str) -> bool:
0563	    return any(token in body for token in ("自杀", "自残", "不想活", "伤害自己"))

0566	def _decode_cursor(cursor: str | None) -> int:
0567	    if cursor is None:
0568	        return 0
0569	    try:
0570	        value = int(cursor)
0571	    except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
0572	        raise ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED") from error
0573	    if value < 0:
0574	        raise ApiException(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")

0575	    return value

0579	    response: TreeholeResponseDocument, *, viewer_id: str, public: bool
0580	) -> TreeholeResponseProjection:
0581	    mine = response.author_user_id == viewer_id
0582	    expose = response.state == "published" or mine
0583	    return TreeholeResponseProjection(

0585	        post_id=response.post_id,
0586	        display_name_snapshot=response.display_name_snapshot if expose else None,
0587	        body_sanitized=response.body_sanitized

0589	        else None,
0590	        state=response.state,
0591	        created_at=response.created_at,
0592	        updated_at=response.updated_at,
0593	        object_version=response.version,
0594	    )

0598	    if value is None:

0600	    error = deserialize_api_error(value)

0602	        raise error

0605	    except Exception as exc:
0606	        raise ApiException(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR") from exc

0246	        self,
0247	        *,
0248	        from_at: datetime | None = None,
0249	        to_at: datetime | None = None,
0250	        resource_type: str | None = None,

0263	            and (to_at is None or event.occurred_at <= to_at)

0270	        page_size = max(1, min(limit, 100))
0271	        page = filtered[offset : offset + page_size]
0272	        next_cursor = str(offset + len(page)) if offset + len(page) < len(filtered) else None
0273	        return page, next_cursor

0377	    def _get_mutable(
0378	        self, task_id: str, object_version: int, admin_id: str, key: str
0379	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
0380	        remembered = self._idempotency.get((admin_id, key))
0381	        if remembered is not None:
0382	            raise ApiException(409, "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT")
0383	        task = self.tasks.get(task_id)
0384	        if task is None or task.get("is_deleted", False):
0385	            raise ApiException(404, "NOT_FOUND")
0386	        if int(task["version"]) != object_version:
0387	            raise ApiException(409, "VERSION_CONFLICT", current_version=task["version"])
0388	        return task

0390	    @staticmethod
0391	    def _decode_cursor(cursor: str | None) -> int:
0392	        if not cursor:
0393	            return 0
0394	        try:
0395	            return max(0, int(cursor))
0396	        except ValueError as error:
0397	            raise ApiException(400, "INVALID_REQUEST") from error

0399	    @staticmethod
0400	    def _in_section(task: dict[str, Any], section: WorkbenchSection, admin_id: str) -> bool:
0401	        state = task["state"]
0402	        if section == "needs_action":
0403	            return state == "needs_action" or (
0404	                state == "claimed" and task.get("assigned_admin_id") == admin_id
0405	            )
0406	        if section == "waiting_other":
0407	            return state == "waiting_other" or (
0408	                state == "claimed" and task.get("assigned_admin_id") != admin_id
0409	            )
0410	        if section == "recent":
0411	            return state in {"completed", "cancelled"}
0412	        return True

0461	        self._idempotency[(admin_id, key)] = result

0465	    ) -> None:
0466	        self.audit.write(
0467	            request_id=request_id,

0470	            capability="super_admin",
0471	            action=action,
0472	            resource_type="task",
0473	            resource_id=task["task_id"],
0474	            data_scope="task_state,object_version",
0475	            outcome=outcome,
0476	            reason_code=None,
0477	            occurred_at=datetime.now(UTC),
0478	            facts={"task_kind": task["task_kind"], "object_version": task["version"]},
0479	        )
